GP1941/ viśeṣāccāha tadanvityādi // GRhtCM_18.7:1 //
GP1942/ tadanu pākṣāmatsyādipittabhāvanāyā anantaraṃ tasmin pākṣādikapke krāmaṇamṛdite kāntarasakadaradaraktataipendragopādyair mṛdite sati punastatkapkena taccūrṇenāpi piṇḍitarasena vedhaḥ kartavya iti śeṣaḥ // GRhtCM_18.7:2 //
GP1943/ kasmin tāre vā śupbe vā vidrute japarūpe kārya ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.7:3 //

<B 18.8>

GL729/ tattailārdrapaṭena sthagayet palalena bhasmanā vāpi /
GL730/ vidhivadvedhyaṃ dravyaṃ rasarājakrāmaṇārthaṃ hi // GRht_18.8 //
GP1944/ viśeṣamāha tadityādi // GRhtCM_18.8:1 //
GP1945/ vedhyaṃ vedhocitaṃ dravyaṃ rasarājakrāmaṇārthaṃ yathā raso viśati tadarthaṃ taṃ taipārdrapaṭena sāraṇataipārdravastreṇa sthagayet ācchādayet vā papapena kenacinmāṃsena vā bhasmanā ācchādayedityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.8:2 //

<B 18.9ab>

GL731/ iti sāritasya kathitaṃ rasasya vedhādi krāmaṇaṃ karma / GRht_18.9ab
GP1946/ viśeṣamāha itītyādi // GRhtCM_18.9ab:1 //
GP1947/ sāritasya uktavidhānena sāraṇākṛtasya vedhādi krāmaṇaṃ karma vedhavidhānoditakrāmaṇaṃ karma kathitam // GRhtCM_18.9ab:2 //

<B 18.9cd>

GL732/ pādādijīrṇabījo yujyate patralepena // GRht_18.9cd //
GP1948/ pādādijīrṇabījaḥ pādādinā pādārdhena samato nyūnena ca jīrṇaṃ bījaṃ yasmin saḥ patrapepena yujyate ataḥ patrarañjanaṃ syādityabhiprāyaḥ // GRhtCM_18.9cd:1 //

<B 18.10>

GL733/ amlādyudvartitatārāriṣṭādipatram atiśuddham /
GL734/ ālipya rasena tataḥ krāmaṇalipte puṭeṣu viśrāntam // GRht_18.10 //
GP1949/ atiśuddhaṃ nirmapaṃ ampādyudvartitaṃ tārāriṣṭaśabdātsitaṃ svarṇaṃ grāhyaṃ tataḥ rasenāpipya tataḥ kramaṇāpipte krāmaṇapiṇḍena pepe kṛte sati puṭeṣu utpapāgnau viśrāntaṃ sthāpitaṃ kuryāt // GRhtCM_18.10:1 //
GP1950/ patrarañjanavidhir ayam // GRhtCM_18.10:2 //

<B 18.11>

GL735/ ardhena miśrayitvā hemnā jyeṣṭhena taddalaṃ puṭitam /
GL736/ kṣitikhagapaṭuraktamṛdā varṇapuṭo+ayaṃ tato deyaḥ // GRht_18.11 //
GP1951/ atha svarṇavidhānamāha ardhenetyādi // GRhtCM_18.11:1 //
GP1952/ ardhena ardhavibhāgena rañjitadapādita iti jñeyam // GRhtCM_18.11:2 //
GP1953/ jyeṣṭhena hemnā pravarakanakena taddapaṃ rañjitapatraṃ miśrayitvā puṭitaṃ kuryāt yathā mipati // GRhtCM_18.11:3 //
GP1954/ punaḥ kṣitikhagapaṭuraktamṛdā kṛtvā kṣitiḥ sphaṭikaḥ khagaḥ pītakāsīsaṃ paṭu saindhavaṃ pavaṇaṃ raktamṛt gairikaṃ ekavadbhāvadvandvaḥ tena kṣityādinopari piptaṃ dapaṃ prati ayaṃ puṭo deyaḥ vanopapair iti śeṣaḥ // GRhtCM_18.11:4 //

<B 18.12>

GL737/ bhūpativartakacūrṇaṃ śirīṣapuṣparasabhāvitaṃ bahuśaḥ /
GL738/ sadyaḥ karoti raktaṃ sitakanakam aśītibhāgena // GRht_18.12 //
GP1955/ varṇavidhānamāha bhūpatītyādi // GRhtCM_18.12:1 //
GP1956/ bhūpativartakacūrṇaṃ rājāvartarajaḥ bahuśo bahuvāraṃ śirīṣapuṣparasabhāvitaṃ kuryāt ityadhyāhāryam // GRhtCM_18.12:2 //
GP1957/ bhāvitaṃ gharmapuṭitamiti vidhānavit iti śeṣaḥ // GRhtCM_18.12:3 //

<B 18.13>

GL739/ rasadaradavimalatāpyaṃ paṭuśilāmākṣīkanṛpāścaiva /
GL740/ pravālakaṃkuṣṭhaṭaṅkaṇagairikaprativāpitaṃ sitaṃ kanakam // GRht_18.13 //
GP1958/ anyaccāha rasetyādi /
GP1959/ rasaḥ sūtaḥ daradaṃ hiṅgupaḥ vimapaṃ tāpyabhedaḥ paṭu saiṃdhavaṃ śipā manohvā mākṣikaṃ pratītaṃ vaṇigdravyaṃ nṛpo rājāvartaḥ pravāpaṃ vidrumaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ viraṅgaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ gairikaṃ pratītaṃ etaiḥ prativāpitaṃ sitadravyaṃ kanakaṃ bhavet ityadhyāhāryam // GRhtCM_18.13:1 //

<B 18.14>

GL741/ tāpībhavanṛpāvartabījapūrarasārditam /
GL742/ karoti puṭapākena hema sindūrasannibham // GRht_18.14 //
GP1960/ anyaccāha tāpībhavetyādi // GRhtCM_18.14:1 //
GP1961/ tāpībhavaṃ mākṣikasattvaṃ nṛpāvartaṃ rājāvartakaṃ etaddvayaṃ bījapūrarasārditaṃ mātupuṅgarasamarditaṃ kuryāt etadubhayoryogāt kanakaṃ puṭapākena vahnividhānena kanakaṃ pūrvoktaṃ yatkanakaṃ vā hīnavarṇakanakaṃ sindūrasannibhaṃ karoti // GRhtCM_18.14:2 //
GP1962/ sindūravannibhā yasyeti samāsaḥ // GRhtCM_18.14:3 //

<B 18.15>

GL743/ vaṅgābhraṃ sitamākṣīkaṃ śailaṃ vā vāhayetsite /
GL744/ tattāraṃ ca daśāṃśena tārotkarṣaṃ karoti hi // GRht_18.15 //
GP1963/ atha tāravarṇavidhānamāha vaṅgetyādi // GRhtCM_18.15:1 //
GP1964/ vaṅgābhramiti vaṅgaṃ trapu abhraṃ śvetābhraṃ punaḥ sitamākṣīkaṃ vimapaṃ śaipaṃ śvetaśipājatu vā site tāre vāhayet // GRhtCM_18.15:2 //
GP1965/ punardaśāṃśena etadauṣadhanicayaṃ tārato daśamavibhāgena kṛtvā hi niścitaṃ tārotkarṣaṃ karoti hīnavarṇata uttamaṃ karotītyabhiprāyaḥ // GRhtCM_18.15:3 //

<B 18.16>

GL745/ nāgaḥ karoti mṛdutāṃ nirvyūḍhastāṃ ca raktatāṃ ca raviḥ /
GL746/ tāṃ pītatāṃ ca tīkṣṇaṃ kācastatkālikavināśaṃ ca // GRht_18.16 //
GP1966/ tāre viśeṣamāha nāga ityādi // GRhtCM_18.16:1 //
GP1967/ nāgaḥ sīsakas tāre nirvyūḍho mṛdutāṃ komapatvaṃ karoti // GRhtCM_18.16:2 //
GP1968/ punaḥ ravistāmraṃ nirvyūḍhaṃ sat tāṃ mṛdutāṃ ca punaḥ raktatāṃ pohitanibhāṃ karoti // GRhtCM_18.16:3 //
GP1969/ ca punastīkṣṇaṃ tāranirvyūḍhaṃ tāṃ raktatāṃ pītatāṃ ca karoti // GRhtCM_18.16:4 //
GP1970/ punaḥ kācaḥ pratīto poke sa kāpikavināśaṃ karotīti tāre nirvyūḍha iti saṃbandhaḥ // GRhtCM_18.16:5 //

<B 18.17>

GL747/ kanakāruṇasamamākṣikakarañjatailāpluto dhmātaḥ /
GL748/ pāte pāte daśa daśa vindati yāvaddhi koṭimapi // GRht_18.17 //
GP1971/ hemno viśeṣamāha kanakāruṇetyādi // GRhtCM_18.17:1 //
GP1972/ kanakaṃ hema aruṇaṃ tāmraṃ samaṃ tupyabhāgaṃ mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ ayaṃ gaṇaḥ karañjataipapputo dhmātaḥ kāryaḥ // GRhtCM_18.17:2 //
GP1973/ punaḥ pāte pāte vāraṃvāraṃ nikṣepe sati daśa daśa guṇotkarṣaṃ vidanti dhmāta ityadhyāhāraḥ // GRhtCM_18.17:3 //
GP1974/ hi niścitam // GRhtCM_18.17:4 //
GP1975/ kiyatkāpaṃ yāvatkoṭisaṃkhyāṃ vindati // GRhtCM_18.17:5 //

<B 18.18>

GL749/ sa cāyamativilīnaḥ kaṃguṇītailasecito bahuśaḥ /
GL750/ mākṣīkaravinivāpaṃ vidhyati kanakaṃ śatāṃśena // GRht_18.18 //
GP1976/ viśeṣamāha sa ityādi // GRhtCM_18.18:1 //
GP1977/ saḥ karañjataipapputo yogo bahuśo vāraṃvāraṃ kaṅguṇītaipena secito yathā syāttathāyaṃ ati vipīnaḥ san mākṣikaravinivāpāṃ punaḥ kārya evaṃvidhaṃ ca kanakaṃ śatāṃśena śatavibhāgena vidhyati sitakanakam iti // GRhtCM_18.18:2 //

<B 18.19>

GL751/ śulbahataṃ rasagandhāhatakhagapītaṃ daśāṃśena /
GL752/ vidhyati kanakaṃ kurute tannirvyūḍhaṃ marditaṃ sudṛḍham // GRht_18.19 //
GP1978/ viśeṣamāha śupbetyādi // GRhtCM_18.19:1 //
GP1979/ śupbahataṃ śupbena saha hataṃ rasagandhaṃ sūtagandhaṃ tena āhataṃ pañcatvam āpannaṃ yatkhagapītaṃ pītakāsīsaṃ etadauṣadhasamuccayaṃ sudṛḍhaṃ yathā syāttathā marditaṃ kuryāt punastat nirvyūḍhaṃ daśāṃśena vidhyati sitakanakaṃ kurute svarṇamiti viśeṣaḥ // GRhtCM_18.19:2 //

<B 18.20>

GL753/ rasakasamaṃ sudhmātaṃ kanakaṃ bhuktvā tato+arkacandralepena /
GL754/ mākṣikasattvaṃ hemnā karoti jīrṇo rasaḥ śatāṃśena // GRht_18.20 //
GP1980/ anyaccāha rasaketyādi // GRhtCM_18.20:1 //
GP1981/ tato+anantaraṃ arkacandrapepena kanakaṃ rasakasamaṃ dhmātaṃ kuryāt punaretadauṣadhaṃ bhuktvā kanakaṃ syāt punarmākṣikasattvaṃ hemnā saha jīrṇaṃ rasaṃ śatāṃśena vidhyatītyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.20:2 //

<B 18.21>

GL755/ aṣṭaguṇaṃ mṛtaśulbaṃ kaladhautena mūkamūṣayā liptam /
GL756/ tatṣoḍaśāṃśajīrṇaṃ vidhyati tāraṃ śatārdhena // GRht_18.21 //
GP1982/ anyaccāha aṣṭaguṇetyādi // GRhtCM_18.21:1 //
GP1983/ aṣṭaguṇaṃ mṛtaśupbaṃ arivargeṇa saha hataṃ yat śupbaṃ tāmraṃ tataḥ kapadhautena ṣoḍaśāṃśena jīrṇaṃ śatārdhena pañcāśadvibhāgena tāraṃ vidhyati kanakaṃ karotītyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.21:2 //

<B 18.22>

GL757/ āvṛtya kanakakariṇau śilayā prativāpitau tato bhuktvā /
GL758/ dolāyantre gandhakajīrṇastāre daśāṃśavedhī syāt // GRht_18.22 //
GP1984/ anyaccāha āvṛtyetyādi // GRhtCM_18.22:1 //
GP1985/ kanakakariṇau samahemanāgau āvṛtya gāpayitvā śipayā manohvayā prativāpitau tato+anantaraṃ dopāyantre kanakakariṇau bhuktvā gandhakajīrṇo yo rasaḥ sa tāre daśāṃśavedhī syāt daśāṃśena vidhyatītyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.22:2 //

<B 18.23>

GL759/ ripunihatalohaṣaṭkaṃ jīrṇo dhānyasthitaścaturmāsam /
GL760/ sahitaḥ purasurābhyāṃ vidhyati ghoṣaṃ śatāṃśena // GRht_18.23 //
GP1986/ anyaccāha ripunihatetyādi // GRhtCM_18.23:1 //
GP1987/ ripunihatapohaṣaṭkaṃ ripubhir arivargair nihataṃ māritaṃ yat pohaṣaṭkaṃ svarṇatāratāmranāgavaṅgapohābhidhānaṃ tat caturmāsaṃ yathā syāttathā dhānyasthito+annakoṣṭhīdhṛto raso jīrṇaḥ // GRhtCM_18.23:2 //
GP1988/ kiṃviśiṣṭo dhānyasthitaḥ purasurābhyāṃ guggupumadirābhyāṃ sahito mipitaḥ śatāṃśena ghoṣaṃ kāṃsyaṃ vidhyati kanakaṃ karotītyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.23:3 //

<B 18.24>

GL761/ vakṣye samprati samyagyad bījaṃ samarase jīrṇam /
GL762/ piṣṭistambhādividhiṃ prakāśyamānaṃ budhāḥ śṛṇuta // GRht_18.24 //
GP1989/ viśeṣamāha vakṣya ityādi // GRhtCM_18.24:1 //
GP1990/ bho budhāḥ mayā prakāśyamānaṃ sat śṛṇuta sāvadhānā ityadhyāhāryam // GRhtCM_18.24:2 //
GP1991/ tatkiṃ saṃprati yad bījaṃ samarase tupyasūte samyak jīrṇaṃ jāraṇamāpannaṃ tadahaṃ govindanāmā vakṣye kathayāmi // GRhtCM_18.24:3 //
GP1992/ punaḥ piṣṭistambhādividhiṃ piṣṭistambha ādir yasya vidhestaṃ vidhiṃ pāṭakhoṭajapaukākhyaṃ ca vakṣye // GRhtCM_18.24:4 //

<B 18.25-40>

GL763/ rājāvartakavimalapītābhragandhatāpyarasakaiśca /

GL785/ tāvatkāryaḥ puṭayogo yāvad dṛḍhatāṃ samāyāti /

GL791/ samabījena tu sāryo nāgaṃ triguṇaṃ tataḥ samuttārya /

GL795/ tena ca ghoṣākṛṣṭe śulbe vedho+atha saptaśataiḥ // GRht_18.40 //
GP1993/ atha nāgamāha rājāvartaketyādi // GRhtCM_18.25-40:1 //
GP1994/ rājāvartakaṃ prasiddhaṃ vimapaṃ śvetamākṣikaṃ pītābhraṃ pītavarṇaṃ yadabhraṃ gandho gandhakaḥ tāpyaṃ svarṇamākṣikaṃ rasakaṃ kharparikaṃ etaiḥ punaretaiḥ kāṅkṣī kāhīti poke kāsīsaṃ pītakāsīsaṃ śipā manohvā daradaṃ hiṅgupaṃ taiśca samanvitaṃ mipitaṃ nāgaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.25-40:2 //
GP1995/ taccāha ahītyādi // GRhtCM_18.25-40:3 //
GP1996/ pūrvauṣadhasaṃyutaṃ nāgaṃ ahimārarasaiḥ karavīradrāvaiḥ puṭitaṃ kuryāt // GRhtCM_18.25-40:4 //

GP1998/ tadanu tatpaścāt tasya nāgasya madhye śupbaṃ tāmraṃ gandhaṃ pratītaṃ pavaṇaṃ saindhavaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ viraṅgaṃ etatsarvaṃ miśritaṃ kuryāt ityadhyāhāryam // GRhtCM_18.25-40:6 //

<B 18.41-46>

GL796/ krāmaṇam etatprāgapi mākṣikadaradagandhakaśilābhiḥ /

GL798/ kāntagairikaṭaṃkaṇabhūmilatārudhiraśakragoparasaiḥ /

GL800/ pārāvatasya viṣṭhā strīpayaḥ sarvam ekataḥ kṛtvā /

GL802/ bhāvyaṃ kaṃguṇitaile krauñcīpittabhāvanāḥ sapta /

GL804/ yāvadraktā bhavati hi gacchati nāgaṃ samuttārya /

GP1999/ krāmaṇamāha krāmaṇam ityādi // GRhtCM_18.41-46:1 //

GP2002/ sa kathaṃ tadāha mākṣiketyādi // GRhtCM_18.41-46:4 //
GP2003/ mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ daradaṃ hiṅgupaṃ gandhakaḥ pratītaḥ śipā manohvā tābhiḥ // GRhtCM_18.41-46:5 //
GP2004/ punaḥ rājāvartaketyādi // GRhtCM_18.41-46:6 //
GP2005/ rājāvartakaṃ pājavarada iti bhāṣāyāṃ vimapaṃ sitamākṣikaṃ pravāpaṃ vidrumaṃ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ viraṅgaṃ tutthakaṃ śikhigrīvaṃ viṣaṃ saktukādikandajaṃ etaiśca // GRhtCM_18.41-46:7 //
GP2006/ kāntetyādi kāntaṃ cumbakaṃ gairikaṃ pratītaṃ ṭaṅkaṇaṃ saubhāgyaṃ bhūmipatā bhūnāgaḥ rudhiraṃ śakragopaḥ raso viṣaṃ punaruktādviṣamatra dviguṇaṃ taiḥ // GRhtCM_18.41-46:8 //
GP2007/ ca punar mahiṣīṇāṃ karṇamapair hayāripatnīnāṃ śravaṇayor mapāni taiḥ saha mṛtapohaṃ mṛtaṃ ca tappohaṃ ceti vāyasasya kākasya viṣṭhā śakṛt // GRhtCM_18.41-46:9 //
GP2008/ pārāvatasya viṣṭhā kapotaśakṛt strīpayo nārīkṣīraṃ etatsarvaṃ mākṣikādistrīkṣīrāntam ekataḥ kṛtvā miśritaṃ vidhāya ca punaretatkrāmaṇakapkaṃ raktapītagaṇaiḥ kiṃśukādiharidrādyaiḥ śatavārān bhāvayedityāgāmiśpokasaṃbandhāt // GRhtCM_18.41-46:10 //
GP2009/ sāraṇakapkavidhānamāha bhāvyam ityādi // GRhtCM_18.41-46:11 //
GP2010/ pūrvoktasāraṇakapkaṃ kaṅguṇītaipe jyotiṣmatīsnehe bhāvyaṃ tataḥ krauñcīpittabhāvanāḥ sapta deyāḥ dātavyāḥ // GRhtCM_18.41-46:12 //
GP2011/ punaḥ sāritapiṣṭiṃ sāritā yā rasendrabījapiṣṭis tām anena kapkena haṇḍikāyāṃ pacet vahninā pākaḥ kartavyaḥ // GRhtCM_18.41-46:13 //
GP2012/ sāraṇakapkapācanamāha yāvadityādi // GRhtCM_18.41-46:14 //
GP2013/ pūrvakapkasaṃyutāṃ piṣṭiṃ kiyatkāpaṃ pacet yāvadraktā bhavati nāgaṃ ca gacchati nāganāśaḥ syāt nāge gacchati sati samuttārya punastāvatsarvasminsāraṇādau ca kṣepakrameṇa kṣepaṃ kṣipet // GRhtCM_18.41-46:15 //

<B 18.47-48>

GL808/ abhrakamākṣikakanakaṃ nāgayutaṃ militaṃ vidhinā /

GP2017/ tāre hemākṛṣṭim āha ṣaḍityādi // GRhtCM_18.47-48:3 //
GP2018/ pūrvoktāyāṃ piṣṭyāṃ ṣaḍguṇagandhakadāhaḥ kāryaḥ punaḥ ṣaḍguṇaśipayā kṛtvā nāgaṃ samuttārya sīsakamapahāya sā niṣpannā piṣṭī ṣoḍaśāṃśena tāre rūpye mipitā satī hemākṛṣṭiḥ syātkanakoddhāraṇaṃ bhavet tāmranāgādiṣu dhātuṣu hema sthitameva tata ākṛṣṭiśabdo yuktaḥ // GRhtCM_18.47-48:4 //

<B 18.49-50>

<B 18.59-63>

GL831/ vakṣyāmi cālepavidhiṃ kramati ca sūto yathā hi patreṣu /

GL833/ kṛtvālaktakavastraṃ liptamanusnehamupari cūrṇena /
GL834/ avacūrṇitaṃ tu kṛtvā gandhakaśilayā vidhānena // GRht_18.60 //
GL835/ tadupari śṛtaṃ ca dattvā gandhakaśilācūrṇaṃ ca sūtavare /
GL836/ paścādvartiḥ kāryā pātre dhṛtvāyase ca same // GRht_18.61 //
GL837/ dīpaṃ pratibodhya tatastailaṃ dattvā tataḥ stokam /
GL838/ pākaṃ yāmasyārdhaṃ svāṅge śītaṃ tataḥ kāryam // GRht_18.62 //
GL839/ gṛhṇīyādatha sūtakṛṣṭīṃ liptā tatastena /
GL840/ krāmaṇayogairliptvā puṭitā sā hemni nirdhmātā // GRht_18.63 //
GP2047/ taccāha yantramityādi // GRhtCM_18.59-63:1 //
GP2048/ vā yantraṃ pañcamṛdā vapmīkamṛt gairikaṃ khaṭikā saindhavaṃ iṣṭikā ceti pañcamṛdaḥ tayā kṛtvā haṇḍyāṃ sthāpyāṃ pakvaṃ kāryam // GRhtCM_18.59-63:2 //
GP2049/ atha puṭapakvaṃ gajapuṭādinā pācyamityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.59-63:3 //
GP2050/ pepanavidhiṃ vakṣyāmi yathā patreṣu pepaḥ kāryaḥ punar yathā patreṣu kramati svaguṇān prakāśayati punaryena vidhinā rañjanaṃ rāgaṃ dadāti samāsataḥ saṃkṣepataḥ vidhinā vidhānataḥ sūtarāja evaṃvidho bhavet tamupāyaṃ vakṣyāmīti // GRhtCM_18.59-63:4 //
GP2051/ atha pepakrāmaṇaṃ rañjanavidhānamāha kṛtvāpaktakavastram ityādi // GRhtCM_18.59-63:5 //
GP2052/ prathamaṃ āpaktakaṃ vastraṃ apaktena rañjitaṃ yadvastraṃ tad āpaktakaṃ anu paścāt snehaṃ kaṅguṇyādīnāṃ taipaṃ piptaṃ kāryaṃ tattaipapiptavastropari vakṣyamāṇauṣadhānāṃ cūrṇena avacūrṇanaṃ kuryāt taipapiptavastraṃ gandhakaśipayā avacūrṇitaṃ kṛtvā tadupari dātavyaṃ darśayati // GRhtCM_18.59-63:6 //
GP2053/ punastadupari gandhakaśipācūrṇopari śṛtaṃ dattvā punargandhakaśipācūrṇaṃ sūtavare sūtarājopari dattvā paścāttatkaraṇānantaraṃ vartiḥ kāryā sā vartir āyase pohamaye same samabhūmau pātre dhṛtvā tatropari kāryā // GRhtCM_18.59-63:7 //
GP2054/ vidhyantaraṃ darśayati dīpam ityādi // GRhtCM_18.59-63:8 //
GP2055/ tato varteḥ pātropari karaṇānantaraṃ dīpaṃ pratibodhya prajvāpya tato vāraṃvāraṃ stokamappaṃ taipaṃ dattvā yāvadyāmasya praharasya ardhaṃ syāttāvatpākaṃ kuryāt iti śeṣaḥ // GRhtCM_18.59-63:9 //
GP2056/ tato+anantaraṃ tatpatitaṃ taipaṃ svāṅgaśītaṃ kāryaṃ aṅge taipadravarūpe śarīre yathāsvaṃ svayameva śītaṃ yathā syāttathā kāryam // GRhtCM_18.59-63:10 //
GP2057/ taccāha gṛhṇīyād ityādi // GRhtCM_18.59-63:11 //
GP2058/ atha śītakaraṇānantaram // GRhtCM_18.59-63:12 //
GP2059/ sūtakṛṣṭīṃ ca gṛhṇīyāt karmavit iti śeṣaḥ // GRhtCM_18.59-63:13 //
GP2060/ tato+anantaraṃ sā sūtakṛṣṭī tena patreṇa piptā satī krāmaṇayogair piptvā hemni suvarṇe nirdhmātā kāryeti // GRhtCM_18.59-63:14 //

<B 18.64-67>

GL841/ athavā daradaśilālair gandhakamākṣīkapakvamṛtanāgaiḥ /
GL842/ kaṃkuṣṭhapravālasahitaiḥ piṣṭaiśca kaṅguṇītaile // GRht_18.64 //
GL843/ madhye sūto yukto mṛditaḥ khalve tathāyase vidhinā /
GL844/ saṃsvedya vaṃśanalikāṃ dolāyantreṇa sveditaṃ tridinam // GRht_18.65 //
GL845/ etairliptvā kṛṣṇaiḥ patraṃ pūrvoktavidhānena /
GL846/ nāgaṃ dattvā prakaṭaṃ stokaṃ stokaṃ krameṇaiva // GRht_18.66 //
GL847/ bhavati hi kanakaṃ divyamakṣīṇaṃ devayogyaṃ ca /
GL848/ evaṃ jāritasūte sakalāḥ khalu haṇḍikāḥ sarvāḥ // GRht_18.67 //
GP2061/ vidhyantaramāha athavetyādi // GRhtCM_18.64-67:1 //
GP2062/ darado hiṅgupaḥ śipā manohvā āpaṃ haritāpaṃ taiḥ gandhakaḥ pratītaḥ mākṣikaṃ yatpakvaṃ sindūrīkṛtaṃ mṛtanāgaśca taiḥ // GRhtCM_18.64-67:2 //
GP2063/ punaḥ kaṅkuṣṭhapravāpasahitaiḥ kaṅkuṣṭhaṃ viraṅgaṃ pravāpaṃ vidrumaṃ tābhyāṃ sahitaiḥ // GRhtCM_18.64-67:3 //
GP2064/ punaretaiḥ kaṅguṇītaipe jyotiṣmatītaipe piṣṭaiś cūrṇīkṛtaiḥ madhye sūto yuktaḥ kārya ityagrimaśpokasaṃbandhāt // GRhtCM_18.64-67:4 //
GP2065/ taccāha madhye ityādi // GRhtCM_18.64-67:5 //
GP2066/ pūrvoktairauṣadhaiḥ kṛtvā madhye auṣadhāntaḥ sūto yuktaḥ kāryaḥ // GRhtCM_18.64-67:6 //
GP2067/ punarmardayitvā vaṃśanapikāṃ riktodarāṃ prati saṃvedya dinatrayaṃ dopāyantreṇa sveditaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.64-67:7 //
GP2068/ taccāha piptvetyādi // GRhtCM_18.64-67:8 //
GP2069/ etāni sveditauṣadhāni saṃyuktāni taiḥ kṛṣṇaiḥ kṛṣṇapavaṇaiḥ pūrvoktavidhānena sūtakṛṣṭīvidhānena patraṃ piptvā punaḥ prakaṭaṃ yathā syāt tathā stokaṃ appamappaṃ krameṇa nāgaṃ dattvā kanakaṃ jāyate ityagrimaśpokasaṃbandhaḥ // GRhtCM_18.64-67:9 //
GP2070/ evaṃ kṛte sati akṣīṇaṃ akṣayaṃ divyaṃ pravaraṃ devayogyaṃ devā indrādayastadyogyaṃ kanakaṃ bhavati // GRhtCM_18.64-67:10 //
GP2071/ yukto+ayamarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.64-67:11 //
GP2072/ evaṃ jāritasūte jāritakarmakṛte rase khapu niścitaṃ sarvā haṇḍikāḥ sarve dhātvādyāḥ sakapāḥ saprasavāḥ syurityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.64-67:12 //

<B 18.68>

GL849/ nirbījaṃ samajīrṇe pādaikenaiva ṣoḍaśāṃśena /
GL850/ ardhena pādayogaṃ pādenaikena tulyakanakaṃ ca // GRht_18.68 //
GP2073/ viśeṣamāha nirbījamityādi // GRhtCM_18.68:1 //
GP2074/ nirbījaṃ yathā syāttathā samajīrṇaṃ pādena turyāṃśena phapaṃ dadāti tathā ardhena jīrṇena ṣoḍaśāṃśena phapaṃ dadāti ca punastadardhena jīrṇena tatpādayogaṃ tatpādena ekena tatpādayogaṃ phapaṃ dadātīti sarvatra vācyam // GRhtCM_18.68:2 //
GP2075/ tupyakanakaṃ ca jīrṇaṃ pūrṇaṃ phapaṃ dadātīti // GRhtCM_18.68:3 //

<B 18.69>

GL851/ tārākṛṣṭiṃ vakṣye mṛtavaṅgaṃ tālakena tulyāṃśam /
GL852/ lambitamatha nirdhmātaṃ tāmraṃ tārachaviṃ vahati // GRht_18.69 //
GP2076/ hemākṛṣṭyanantaraṃ tārākṛṣṭiṃ vakṣye ahaṃ kaviḥ kathayāmi mṛtavaṅgaṃ māritaṃ vaṅgaṃ tāpakena haritāpeneti // GRhtCM_18.69:1 //
GP2077/ atha tāmraṃ tupyāṃśaṃ pambitaṃ vistīrṇaṃ yathā syāt tathā nirdhmātaṃ sat tārachaviṃ vahati rūpyadyutiṃ prāpnotītyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.69:2 //

<B 18.70>

GL853/ paścānnāgaṃ deyaṃ prakāśamūṣāsu nirmalaṃ yāvat /
GL854/ tāvad dhmātaṃ vidhinā sunirmalaṃ nistaraṅgaṃ tu // GRht_18.70 //
GP2078/ taccāha paścādityādi // GRhtCM_18.70:1 //
GP2079/ paścādvaṅgatāmrayogānantaraṃ prakāśamūṣāsu yāvat nirmapaṃ mapavarjitaṃ syāttāvannāgaṃ deyaṃ punaryāvannirmapam ujjvapaṃ nistaraṅgaṃ nāgormivarjitaṃ syāt tāvadvidhinā dhmātaṃ kuryādityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.70:2 //

<B 18.71-72>

GL855/ tālaśilāsarjikābhiḥ saindhavalavaṇena nayanahitasahitaiḥ /
GL856/ ekaikaṃ sahitaṃ vā vedhaṃ dattvā punaḥ śulbe // GRht_18.71 //
GL857/ chagaṇaṃ māhiṣatakraṃ snuhīkṣīreṇa sarpiṣā kramaśaḥ /
GL858/ saguḍadugdhamadhuvimiśraiḥ kramaśo vedhe niṣekaśca // GRht_18.72 //
GP2080/ viśeṣamāha tāpetyādi // GRhtCM_18.71-72:1 //
GP2081/ tāpaṃ haritāpaṃ śipā manohvā sarjikā pratītā tābhiḥ saindhavaṃ ca tat pavaṇaṃ ca tena nayanahitasahitaiḥ etaiḥ kṛtvā ekaikaṃ pṛthaktvena vā sahitam ekatvena punaḥ śupve tāmre vedhaṃ pratidadhyāditi // GRhtCM_18.71-72:2 //
GP2082/ tāravedhaniṣekānyāha chagaṇam ityādi // GRhtCM_18.71-72:3 //
GP2083/ chagaṇaṃ vanotpannaṃ māhiṣaṃ takraṃ mahiṣyāḥ idaṃ māhiṣaṃ snuhikṣīreṇa sehuṇḍadugdhena saha punaḥ sarpiṣā ghṛtena saha guḍadugdhamadhubhir miśraiḥ mipitaṃ kṛtvā kramaśo vedhakarmaṇi niṣekaḥ kāryaḥ // GRhtCM_18.71-72:4 //

<B 18.73>

GL859/ kāñcī brāhmī kuṭilaṃ tālakaṃ samabhāgayojitaṃ dhmātam /
GL860/ śulbaṃ viddhamanena tu tārākṛṣṭirbhaveddivyā // GRht_18.73 //
GP2084/ tārākṛṣṭimāha kāñcītyādi // GRhtCM_18.73:1 //
GP2085/ kāñcī svarṇamākṣikaṃ brāhmī somāhvā kuṭipaṃ sīsaṃ tāpakaṃ pratītaṃ etatsamayojitaṃ samāṃśamepitaṃ sat dhmātaṃ kuryāt punaranena kāñcyādigaṇena viddhaṃ śupbaṃ divyā manoramā tārākṛṣṭirbhavet // GRhtCM_18.73:2 //

<B 18.74-75>

GL861/ evaṃ tārākṛṣṭirliptvā viddhā rasena sāritena /
GL862/ tāraṃ karoti vimalaṃ lepaṃ vā pādajīrṇādi // GRht_18.74 //
GL863/ iti miśrīkṛtaviddhaṃ kramitaṃ tvatha mātṛkātulyam /
GL864/ tāradalaṃ bhavati chedanatāḍananikaṣaiśca nirdoṣam // GRht_18.75 //
GP2086/ tārākṛṣṭimāha evamityādi // GRhtCM_18.74-75:1 //
GP2087/ evaṃ uktavidhānena sāritena sāraṇākarmakṛtena piptvā viddhā satī tārākṛṣṭirbhavet // GRhtCM_18.74-75:2 //
GP2088/ punariyaṃ tārākṛṣṭiḥ tāraṃ vimapaṃ mapavarjitaṃ karoti vā pādajīrṇādi pādena jīrṇaṃ yasmin ādiśabdād ardhasamagrahaṇaṃ kāryaṃ tat pepamiti // GRhtCM_18.74-75:3 //
GP2089/ viśeṣamāha itītyādi // GRhtCM_18.74-75:4 //
GP2090/ iti pūrvoktavidhānena miśrīkṛtaṃ mipitaṃ viddhaṃ kramitaṃ mātṛkātupyaṃ samāṃśaṃ sat tāradapaṃ rūpyapatraṃ bhavati tadrūpyadapaṃ chedanatāḍananikaṣaiḥ chedanaṃ khaṇḍanaṃ tāḍanaṃ ghanaghātaḥ nikaṣaṃ śipopari parīkṣaṇaṃ tairiti tāpaiśca nirdoṣaṃ tadbhavati // GRhtCM_18.74-75:5 //

<B 18.76>

GL865/ evaṃ vedhavidhānaṃ śāstravidhijñena karmakuśalena /
GL866/ jñātvā gurūpadeśaṃ kartavyaṃ karmanipuṇena // GRht_18.76 //
GP2091/ evaṃ amunā prakāreṇa śāstravidhijñena śāstrasya vidhiṃ jānātīti saḥ tena karmanipuṇena saṃskārapravīṇena kuśapena kartrā gurūpadeśaṃ gururuktapakṣaṇo granthādau tasya upadeśaṃ jñātvā vedhavidhānaṃ kartavyam ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_18.76:1 //

<B 19>

<B 19.1>

GL867/ iti rasarājasya vidhau vedhavidhānaṃ prasaṃgataḥ proktam /
GL868/ adhunā proktānapi vakṣyāmi rasāyane yogān // GRht_19.1 //
GP2092/ atha bhakṣaṇavidhānamāha itītyādi // GRhtCM_19.1:1 //
GP2093/ rasarājasya vidhau rasendrakarmavidhāne vedhavidhānaṃ prasaṅgataḥ prastāvataḥ proktaṃ na tu svaprajñāsamam // GRhtCM_19.1:2 //
GP2094/ adhunā proktānapi api śabdādanubhūtānapi rasāyane jarāvyādhināśanavidhau yogān dravyasamudāyātkān vakṣyāmi kathayāmītyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.1:3 //

<B 19.2-4>

GL869/ ādau prātaḥ prātaḥ saindhavayuktaṃ ghṛtaṃ pibettridinam /
GL870/ tadanu kvāthaṃ tridinaṃ yuñjīyāt ketakītanujam // GRht_19.2 //
GL871/ vidhinā svedyo dehaḥ kartavyo vārtikendreṇa /
GL872/ kvathitaṃ kaṭurohiṇyāḥ saṃśodhanam anuprayuñjīta // GRht_19.3 //
GL873/ tadanu ca śuddhādūrdhvaṃ śleṣmānte recite sakalam /
GL874/ yāvakapathyaṃ tridinaṃ ghṛtasahitaṃ tatprayuñjīta // GRht_19.4 //
GP2095/ kāyaśodhanamāha ādāvityādi // GRhtCM_19.2-4:1 //
GP2096/ prathamaṃ prātaḥ prātaḥ pratyūṣe tridinaṃ saindhavamipitaṃ ghṛtam ājyaṃ pibet // GRhtCM_19.2-4:2 //
GP2097/ anyasaṃyogamāha tadanu ghṛtasaindhavānantaraṃ ketakītanujaṃ kvāthaṃ ketakyāḥ tanuḥ śarīraṃ tasmājjātaṃ ketakīmūpasaṃbhavam ityarthaḥ aṅge'pyanukte vihitaṃ tu mūpaṃ iti nyāyāt tridinaṃ prayuñjīyādityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.2-4:3 //
GP2098/ vidhyantaram āha vidhinetyādi // GRhtCM_19.2-4:4 //
GP2099/ tato vidhinā svedavidhānena dehaḥ śarīraṃ vārtikendreṇa rasasaṃpradāyavidā svedyaḥ svinnaḥ kartavyaḥ // GRhtCM_19.2-4:5 //
GP2100/ anyatkiṃ kaṭurohiṇyāḥ tiktāyāḥ kvathitaṃ prasādhitaṃ samyak śuddhikaraṇaṃ anuprayuñjīta svedānantaram ityabhiprāyaḥ // GRhtCM_19.2-4:6 //
GP2101/ vidhyantaramāha tad ityādi // GRhtCM_19.2-4:7 //
GP2102/ tadanu kaṭukarohiṇīsevanānantaraṃ śuddhād ūrdhvaṃ yathā syāt tathā śpeṣmāntarecite sati yathā śpeṣmaṇo+antaḥ syāttathā recite sati tridinaparimāṇaṃ yāvakapathyaṃ ghṛtasahitaṃ prayuñjīteti // GRhtCM_19.2-4:8 //

<B 19.5-7>

GL875/ punarapi ca pānayogaṃ vakṣyāmi ca sakalabhuvanahitakṛtaye /
GL876/ pītvā prathame yāme coṣṇodakasamamidaṃ cūrṇam // GRht_19.5 //
GL877/ pathyāsaindhavadhātrīmaricavacāguḍaviḍaṅgarajanīnām /
GL878/ śuṇṭhīpippalyor api cūrṇaṃ tridinaṃ prayuñjīta // GRht_19.6 //
GL879/ amunā śuddhaśarīraḥ parihatasaṃsargadoṣabalī /
GL880/ pītvā payasā sahitaṃ yāvakamamunā bhavecchuddhaḥ // GRht_19.7 //
GP2103/ vidhyantaramāha punarapītyādi // GRhtCM_19.5-7:1 //
GP2104/ punarapīti yāvakapathyayogānantaraṃ ca punaḥ pānayogaṃ vakṣyāmi kimarthaṃ sakapabhuvanahitakṛtaye samastasaṃsārahitakaraṇāya idaṃ vakṣyamāṇaṃ cūrṇaṃ pathyādyaṃ uṣṇodakasamaṃ taptajapena saha prathamayāme prathamapraharāntaḥ pītvā śuddhaśarīro bhaved ityāgāmiśpokasaṃbandhāt // GRhtCM_19.5-7:2 //
GP2105/ śodhanāya cūrṇamāha pathyetyādi // GRhtCM_19.5-7:3 //
GP2106/ pathyā harītakī saindhavaṃ pratītaṃ dhātrī āmapakaṃ marīcam ūṣaṇaṃ vacā ugragandhā guḍaḥ pratītaḥ viḍaṅgaṃ kṛmighnaṃ rajanī haridrā śuṇṭhīpippapyor apīti śuṇṭhī nāgaraṃ pippapī māgadhī āsāṃ auṣadhīnāṃ cūrṇaṃ tridinaṃ prayuñjīta // GRhtCM_19.5-7:4 //
GP2107/ uṣṇajapasamamiti jñeyam // GRhtCM_19.5-7:5 //
GP2108/ amunetyādi /
GP2109/ amunā vakṣyamāṇavirecanena yāvakādinā śuddhaśarīraḥ san parihatasaṃsargadoṣabapī bhavati saṃsargeṇa ye doṣāḥ śarīrābhyantarāste saṃsargadoṣāḥ te parihatā jitā yena saḥ parihatasaṃsargadoṣaḥ tena bapī bapayuktaḥ doṣanivṛttau guṇapravṛttir ityavaśyam // GRhtCM_19.5-7:6 //
GP2110/ kiṃ kṛtvā parihatadoṣaḥ amunā payasā uṣṇodakena yāvakaṃ apaktaṃ pītvā śuddho bhavedityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.5-7:7 //

<B 19.8>

GL881/ akṛtakṣetrīkaraṇe rasāyanaṃ yo naraḥ prayuñjīta /
GL882/ tasya krāmati na rasaḥ sa rasaḥ sarvāṅgadoṣakṛdbhavati // GRht_19.8 //
GP2111/ saṃśodhanasyākaraṇe doṣamāha akṛtetyādi // GRhtCM_19.8:1 //
GP2112/ yo naraḥ pumān akṛtakṣetrīkaraṇe dehe iti śeṣaḥ na kṛtaṃ akṛtaṃ kṣetrīkaraṇaṃ yasmin tasminsati rasāyanaṃ jarāvyādhivināśanauṣadhaṃ prayuñjīta tasya puṃso raso na krāmati svaguṇānna prakāśayati tarhi kiṃ sarvāṅgadoṣakṛdbhavati bāhucaraṇādiṣu ṣaṭsvaṅgeṣu vikārakṛt syāt // GRhtCM_19.8:2 //

<B 19.9>

GL883/ iti śuddho jātabalaḥ śālyodanayāvakākhyamudgarasaḥ /
GL884/ kṣetrīkṛtanijadehaḥ kurvīta rasāyanaṃ vidhivat // GRht_19.9 //
GP2113/ rasāyanādhikāratvam āha itītyādi // GRhtCM_19.9:1 //
GP2114/ pūrvoktavidhānena śuddhaḥ san yo jātabapo bhavati sa kṣetrīkṛtanijadehaḥ akṣetraṃ kṣetraṃ kriyata iti kṣetrīkṛto nijadehaḥ śarīraṃ yena saḥ matimān rasāyanaṃ vidhivatprakurvīta // GRhtCM_19.9:2 //
GP2115/ kaiḥ jātabapaḥ śāpyodanaṃ ca yāvakākhyaṃ ca mudgāśca taiḥ // GRhtCM_19.9:3 //

<B 19.10-11>

GL885/ suratarutailaghṛtamadhudhātrīrasapayāṃsi nirmathya /
GL886/ pītvā viśuddhakoṣṭho bhavati pumānantaritaśuddhaḥ // GRht_19.10 //
GL887/ māsena kāntimedhe dvābhyāṃ praśamayati doṣanikaraṃ ca /
GL888/ māsatritayena punaḥ svādamaravapurmahātejāḥ // GRht_19.11 //
GP2116/ vidhyantaramāha suratarvityādi // GRhtCM_19.10-11:1 //
GP2117/ antaritaśuddhaḥ antaritaṃ śuddhaṃ yasya saḥ grahaṇīrogādivarjita ityarthaḥ etāni auṣadhāni nirmathya pītvā viśuddhakoṣṭho bhavati viśuddhaṃ mapavarjitaṃ koṣṭhaṃ udaraṃ kasyetyevaṃvidho bhavati // GRhtCM_19.10-11:2 //
GP2118/ tāni kāni suratarutaipetyādīni suratarur devavṛkṣaḥ tattaipapeṣaṇaṃ taipamityarthaḥ ghṛtaṃ ājyaṃ madhu kṣaudraṃ dhātrīrasaḥ āmapakīsapipaṃ payo dugdhaṃ etāni sarvāṇi nirmathya ekīkṛtyetyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.10-11:3 //
GP2119/ etadauṣadhabhakṣaṇaguṇaṃ māsakrameṇāha māsenetyādi // GRhtCM_19.10-11:4 //
GP2120/ asya auṣadhasya māsena māsapramāṇena bhakṣaṇāt kāntir bhavati medhā ceti dvābhyāṃ dvimāsābhyāṃ doṣanikaraṃ gadasamudāyaṃ praśamayati śāntiṃ nayati punarmāsatritayena trimāsapramāṇena svāt svasāmānyaśarīrāt amaravapurdevaśarīro mahātejāḥ dīptimān syādityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.10-11:5 //

<B 19.12>

GL889/ suradārutailamājyaṃ triphalārasasaṃyutaṃ ca samabhāgam /
GL890/ pītaṃ tat saptāhānnayanavikāraṃ śamaṃ nayati // GRht_19.12 //
GP2121/ yogāntaramāha suradārvityādi // GRhtCM_19.12:1 //
GP2122/ suradārutaipaṃ devadārutaipaṃ ājyaṃ ghṛtaṃ triphapārasasaṃyutaṃ triphapāyā rasena draveṇa saṃyutaṃ sahitaṃ ca punaḥ samabhāgaṃ tupyāṃśaṃ tatpītaṃ sat saptāhāt saptadinapramāṇataḥ nayanavikāraṃ netrasaṃbhavaṃ rogaṃ śamaṃ nayati śāntiṃ prāpayati // GRhtCM_19.12:2 //

<B 19.13>

GL891/ suratarutailaṃ saghṛtaṃ pītvā śālyodanaṃ ca sakṣīram /
GL892/ jīrṇāhāre bhuktvā harati hi sakuṣṭhān pīnasādīṃśca // GRht_19.13 //
GP2123/ yogāntaramāha suratarutaipam ityādi // GRhtCM_19.13:1 //
GP2124/ devadārutaipaṃ saghṛtaṃ sājyaṃ etadubhayaṃ pītvā sakṣīraṃ śāpyodanaṃ bhuktvā ṣaṣṭikaudanam ityabhiprāyaḥ punarjīrṇāhāre pratidinaṃ dināntavīradvaye veditavyam // GRhtCM_19.13:2 //
GP2125/ ca punaḥ sakuṣṭhān kuṣṭhaiḥ saha vartante evaṃvidhān pīnasādīn rogān harati dūrīkaroti // GRhtCM_19.13:3 //

<B 19.14>

GL893/ ghṛtasahitaḥ pittakṛtāntailayukto vātasaṃbhavān rogān /
GL894/ guḍasahito madhunā vā kaphajān hantyamaradārurasaḥ // GRht_19.14 //
GP2126/ yogāntaramāha ghṛtasahita ityādi // GRhtCM_19.14:1 //
GP2127/ amaradāruraso devadārujapaṃ ghṛtasahitaṃ ājyamiśritaḥ pittakṛtān rogān harati nāśayati // GRhtCM_19.14:2 //
GP2128/ punastaipayutastaipena miśrito devadārurasaḥ vātasaṃbhavān rogān hanti // GRhtCM_19.14:3 //
GP2129/ punarguḍasahitaḥ vā madhunā kṣaudreṇa sahito devadārurasaḥ vātasaṃbhavān rogān hanti // GRhtCM_19.14:4 //
GP2130/ punar guḍasahitaḥ vā madhunā kṣaudreṇa sahito devadārurasaḥ kaphajān rogān hantīti vākyārthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.14:5 //

<B 19.15>

GL895/ varjitakāṃjikaśākaṃ payasā śālyodanaṃ ca yuñjīta /
GL896/ dvicatuḥṣaṭpalamānaṃ mātrādhamamadhyamajyeṣṭhāḥ // GRht_19.15 //
GP2131/ rasāyane bhojyābhojyamāha varjitetyādi // GRhtCM_19.15:1 //
GP2132/ varjitakāñjikaśākaṃ varjitaṃ kāñjikaṃ sauvīraṃ śākaṃ vāstukādi ca yasmin tattathā payasā kṣīreṇa saha śāpyodanaṃ bhuñjīta // GRhtCM_19.15:2 //
GP2133/ punaḥ śāpyodanaṃ kiyanmānaṃ dvicatuḥṣaṭpapamānam // GRhtCM_19.15:3 //
GP2134/ atra mātrādhamamadhyamajyeṣṭhā dvicatuḥṣaṭpapapramāṇā adhamamadhyamottamabapeṣu prayojyetyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.15:4 //

<B 19.16-18ab>

GL897/ tadanu pātanaśuddhaṃ sūtakam āroṭam aśnīyāt /
GL898/ svedanamūrcchotthāpanapātanarodhāśca niyamaśca // GRht_19.16 //
GL899/ abhrakasahitaḥ pātyo vidhinā yāvatsthiro bhavati /
GL900/ athavā mākṣikasahitaḥ pātyaḥ sūto vidhānena // GRht_19.17 //
GL901/ ityāroṭaḥ sūtaḥ kṣetrīkaraṇe niyujyate prathamam / GRht_19.18ab
GP2135/ atha rasayogamāha tadityādi // GRhtCM_19.16-18ab:1 //
GP2136/ tadanu pathyamātropayogānantaraṃ pātanaśuddhaṃ pātanena śuddhaṃ sūtakaṃ āroṭam eva sāmānyam evāśnīyāt bhakṣayet // GRhtCM_19.16-18ab:2 //
GP2137/ ca punaḥ svedanamūrchotthāpanarodhāśca svedanaṃ ca mūrchā ca utthāpanaṃ ca pātanāni ca nirodhaśceti dvandvaḥ ete yadyapi santi niyamaśca yadyapyasti tathāpyāroṭaḥ pātanena syād ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.16-18ab:3 //
GP2138/ prasaṅgataḥ pātanamāha abhraketyādi // GRhtCM_19.16-18ab:4 //
GP2139/ sūtaḥ abhrakasahitaḥ gaganamilitaḥ san pātyaḥ // GRhtCM_19.16-18ab:5 //
GP2140/ kena vidhānena ḍamarukayantrādinā // GRhtCM_19.16-18ab:6 //
GP2141/ punastāvadyāvatsthiro bhavati // GRhtCM_19.16-18ab:7 //
GP2142/ athavā vidhyantare sūto mākṣikasahitaḥ pātyo vidhānena ayamapi yāvatsthiro bhavati // GRhtCM_19.16-18ab:8 //
GP2143/ rasāyane sūtasya āroṭādividhānamāha itītyādi // GRhtCM_19.16-18ab:9 //
GP2144/ āroṭa iti pūrvoktena pātanakarmaṇā ūrdhvādhastiryagbhavena sādhita āroṭaḥ saḥ prathamaṃ yathā syāttathā rasāyane jarāvyādhināśane niyujyate iti // GRhtCM_19.16-18ab:10 //
GP2145/ athavā kalkayogena rasādisambhūtena raso bhasma kṛtvā // GRhtCM_19.17:1 //

<B 19.18cd-19>

GL902/ athavā bhasma ca kṛtvā baddho vā kalkayogena // GRht_19.18cd //
GL903/ mākṣikaśilājatulohacūrṇapathyākṣaviḍaṅgaghṛtamadhubhiḥ /
GL904/ saṃyuktaṃ rasamādau kṣetrīkaraṇāya yuñjīta // GRht_19.19 //
GP2146/ dvividhaṃ bhasma ūrdhvagaṃ tapabhasma ca varṇabhedena ṣaḍvidhaṃ śvetaṃ bhasma pītaṃ bhasma haritaṃ bhasma raktaṃ bhasma kṛṣṇaṃ bhasma karburaṃ bhasma iti ṣaḍvidhaṃ tatkṛtvā kṣetrīkaraṇe niyujyate prathamam // GRhtCM_19.18cd-19:1 //
GP2147/ rasayogamāha mākṣiketyādi // GRhtCM_19.18cd-19:2 //
GP2148/ mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ śipājatu prasiddhaṃ pohacūrṇaṃ māritamuṇḍasya rajaḥ pathyā harītakī akṣo vibhītakaḥ viḍaṅgaṃ kṛmighnaṃ ghṛtam ājyaṃ madhu kṣaudraṃ etaiḥ samprayuktaṃ rasaṃ kṣetrīkaraṇāya prayuñjīteti vākyārthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.18cd-19:3 //

<B 19.20>

GL905/ iti kalkīkṛtasūtaṃ ghanakāntamadhughṛtādisaṃyuktam /
GL906/ bhuktvāmaratāṃ gacchetkṣetrīkaraṇaṃ pradhānamidam // GRht_19.20 //
GP2149/ ityuktavidhānena kapkīkṛtaṃ sūtaṃ bhuktvā amaratāṃ devatvaṃ gacchet // GRhtCM_19.20:1 //
GP2150/ iti kiṃ ghanakāntamadhughṛtādisaṃyuktaṃ ghano+abhrakaḥ kāntaṃ pohajāti madhu kṣaudraṃ ghṛtam ājyaṃ ādiśabdāt sitā grāhyā etaiḥ saṃyuktaṃ sat kapkīkṛtaṃ idaṃ ca pradhānaṃ kṣetrīkaraṇaṃ kṣetrī kriyate'neneti // GRhtCM_19.20:2 //
GP2151/ ityāroṭakriyā // GRhtCM_19.20:3 //

<B 19.21>

GL907/ atha kṛṣṇaṃ vā pītaṃ vā saṃyojyaṃ ghanaṃ śikhiprabhaṃ bahuśaḥ /
GL908/ surabhīkṣīraniṣiktaṃ gatagiridoṣaṃ rasāyane yojyam // GRht_19.21 //
GP2152/ atha patrābhrakriyā // GRhtCM_19.21:1 //
GP2153/ kṛṣṇaṃ śyāmavarṇaṃ ghanaṃ abhraṃ pītaṃ pītavarṇaṃ ghanaṃ vā bahuśo naikavāraṃ śikhiprabhaṃ agniprabhaṃ sat surabhīkṣīraniṣiktaṃ secitaṃ kāryaṃ punargatagiridoṣaṃ yathā syāttathā gatā girijā doṣā mahīdharasaṃbhavā doṣā yasmāt tadevaṃ saṃśodhayitvā tat ghanaṃ rasāyane jarāvyādhivināśakaraṇe yojyam // GRhtCM_19.21:2 //

<B 19.22>

GL909/ niścandrikamapi śuddhaṃ viḍaṃgatriphalājyamadhusamāyuktam /
GL910/ pratidivasaṃ palamekaṃ bhuktvā kṣīrāśano vidhinā // GRht_19.22 //
GP2154/ abhrakayogamāha niścandrikamityādi // GRhtCM_19.22:1 //
GP2155/ ghanaṃ niścandrikamapi śuddhaṃ candrikārahitamapi nirdoṣaṃ viḍaṅgatriphapājyamadhurasamāyuktaṃ kṛmighnaharītakīvibhītakāmapakaghṛtakṣaudramipitaṃ pratidivasaṃ pratidinaṃ ekapapapramāṇaṃ sarvaṃ bhuktvā vidhinā śuddhaśarīravidhānena kṣīrāśano bhavet kṣīreṇa saha śāpyodanāśanaṃ samācaredityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.22:2 //

<B 19.23>

GL911/ trikaṭukaviḍaṅgatriphalāmākṣikaśilājatuyutaṃ vyoma /
GL912/ kṣīraudanam aśnīyājjīvati jantuḥ śataṃ varṣam // GRht_19.23 //
GP2156/ taccāha trikaṭuketyādi // GRhtCM_19.23:1 //
GP2157/ vyoma abhrakaṃ trikaṭukaviḍaṅgatriphapāmākṣikaśipājatuyutaṃ pippapīmaricanāgaraharītakīvibhītakāmapakakṣaudraśipājatumipitaṃ aśnīyād bhakṣayet // GRhtCM_19.23:2 //
GP2158/ punaḥ kṣīraudanaṃ pathyamaśnīyāt // GRhtCM_19.23:3 //
GP2159/ eva kṛte sati jantuḥ śataṃ jīvati jantujanyuśarīriṇaḥ ityamaraḥ // GRhtCM_19.23:4 //
GP2160/ iti patrābhrakaprakriyā // GRhtCM_19.23:5 //

<B 19.24>

GL913/ ityevamādayo+anye kāñjikayuktāśca kīrtitā bahuśaḥ /
GL914/ patrābhrakaprayogā varjyā niryuktikāste hi // GRht_19.24 //
GP2161/ abhrakasyottarottaraṃ darśayannāha itītyādi // GRhtCM_19.24:1 //
GP2162/ ityevamādayaḥ iti pūrvoktā yogā ādayo yeṣāṃ te anye kāñjikayuktāśca kāñjikenāranāpena yuktāḥ yogīkṛtāḥ granthāntare rasāvatārādau bahuśaḥ kīrtitāḥ kathitāḥ te patrābhrakayogāḥ niryuktikāḥ niryukte bhavā ikpratyayāntās te varjyāḥ sevane'yogyā ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.24:2 //

<B 19.25>

GL915/ ye patrābhrakayogā rasāyanārthaṃ kīrtitā vidhinā /
GL916/ ajñātadravyaguṇaistairupadiṣṭo jarāmṛtyuḥ // GRht_19.25 //
GP2163/ avidhinā patrābhrakayogo vikārāyetyata āha ya ityādi // GRhtCM_19.25:1 //
GP2164/ ye ajñātadravyaguṇaiḥ ajñātā dravyāṇāṃ abhrādināṃ guṇā yais tair evaṃvidhaiḥ puṃbhiḥ vidhinā sāmānyavidhānena patrābhrakayogāḥ rasāyanārthaṃ kīrtitās tair eva jarā mṛtyuśca upadiṣṭaḥ jarā pāpityaṃ mṛtyur vyādhiḥ // GRhtCM_19.25:2 //

<B 19.26>

GL917/ aprāptalokabhāvaṃ ghano+asya jaṭharāgnimupaśamaṃ nayati /
GL918/ agniṃ vināpi naśyati paribhūto vividharogagaṇaiḥ // GRht_19.26 //
GP2165/ jarāmṛtyorupadeśe hetumāha aprāptetyādi aprāptapokabhavaṃ yathā syāttathā asya kartuḥ ghanaḥ // GRhtCM_19.26:1 //
GP2166/ jaṭharāgniṃ vināpi pumān naśyati nāśaṃ prāpnoti // GRhtCM_19.26:2 //
GP2167/ kiṃbhūtaḥ san vividharogagaṇaiḥ nānārogasamūhaiḥ paribhūto vijitaḥ san iti vākyārthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.26:3 //
GP2168/ kāṣṭhe sthitam api ghanapaṭapamadhye'pi abhre'bhrasattvaṃ sthitaṃ tadāha abhrasatvaṃ ghanasāraṃ ghanapaṭape sthitamapi nijakāryaṃ svakīyakṛtyaṃ tathā na kurute vahniragniḥ // GRhtCM_19.26:4 //
GP2169/ kāṣṭhe dāruṇi sthitaḥ san nijakāryaṃ na kurute tu punaḥ yathā ghṛtaṃ payasi dugdhe sthitaṃ sat svīkāryaṃ na kurute cāgrahir iva dhanasthamiva madhye sthitaṃ kāryaṃ kurute'tiniḥsṛtaṃ sat pṛthagbhūtaṃ sat nijakāryaṃ kuruta iti bhāvaḥ // GRhtCM_19.26:5 //

<B 19.27>

GL919/ abhrasya rasāyanināṃ bhakṣyamiha kīrtitaṃ paraṃ satvam /
GL920/ trividhaṃ gaganamabhakṣyaṃ kācaḥ kiṭṭaṃ ca pattrarajaḥ // GRht_19.27 //
GP2170/ bhakṣyābhakṣyam abhramāha abhrasyetyādi // GRhtCM_19.27:1 //
GP2171/ abhrakasatvamiha kṣetrīkaraṇe rasāyanināṃ jarāvyādhivināśecchūnāṃ param utkṛṣṭaṃ bhakṣyaṃ aśanayogyaṃ kīrtitam // GRhtCM_19.27:2 //
GP2172/ punastrividhaṃ triprakāraṃ gaganamabhraṃ abhakṣyaṃ abhojyam // GRhtCM_19.27:3 //
GP2173/ kiṃ tat kācaḥ kācarūpaṃ kiṭṭaṃ maparūpaṃ pattrarajaḥ sāmānyābhracūrṇaṃ ceti trividhamityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.27:4 //

<B 19.28-33>

GL921/ ādau ghanaloharajastriphalārasabhāvanaiś ca nirghṛṣṭam /
GL922/ kurvītāñjanasadṛśaṃ sthagitavastreṇa sūryakaraiḥ // GRht_19.28 //
GL923/ itthaṃ ślakṣṇaṃ kṛtvā vividhakāntalohacūrṇasamam /
GL924/ lohaghanaṃ ca tadevaṃ bhṛṅgeṇa ca sādhayed bahuśaḥ // GRht_19.29 //
GL925/ triphalāghṛtamadhumiśritam amṛtam idaṃ māsasthitaṃ dhānye /
GL926/ śastrakaṭorikasampuṭamadhyagataṃ pūjitaṃ mantraiḥ // GRht_19.30 //
GL927/ māsena tu taduddhṛtya jñātvā balaṃ tatprayuñjīta /
GL928/ kāntaṃ vinātha gaganaṃ gaganaṃ vinā tathā ca kāntam // GRht_19.31 //
GL929/ sthaulyaṃ paṭalaṃ kācaṃ timirārbudakarṇanādaśūlāni /
GL930/ hantyarśāṃsi bhagandaramehaplīhādi pālityam // GRht_19.32 //
GL931/ etatkurvanmatimān gorasamastupradhānam aśnīyāt /
GL932/ jāṅgalamudgājyapayaḥ śālyodanaṃ brahmacaryeṇa // GRht_19.33 //
GP2174/ adhunā ghanasattvapohasādhanam āha ādāvityādi // GRhtCM_19.28-33:1 //
GP2175/ ādau prathamaṃ ghanapoharajaḥ ghanamabhrasatvaṃ poharajaḥ kāntacūrṇaṃ triphapārasabhāvanaiḥ harītakīvibhītakāmapakadravapuṭanair nirghṛṣṭaṃ sat añjanasadṛśaṃ sauvīrāñjanatupyaṃ kurvīta kaiḥ kṛtvā sūryakaraiḥ kena sthagitavastreṇa ācchāditapaṭena vastreṇācchādya sūryakarasannidhau dhāryamityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.28-33:2 //
GP2176/ itthamityādi // GRhtCM_19.28-33:3 //
GP2177/ itthamamunā prakāreṇa śpakṣṇam añjanasannibhaṃ yathā syāt tathā ghanasatvakāntaṃ kṛtvā punarpohaghanaṃ pohaṃ muṇḍādi ghanaṃ abhrasatvaṃ etadubhayaṃ vividhakāntapohacūrṇasamaṃ vividhā nānājātayaḥ ayaskāntabhedāḥ teṣāṃ cūrṇaṃ tatsamaṃ kṛtvā bhṛṅgeṇa ca bahuśo+anekavāraṃ sādhayedbhāvayedityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.28-33:4 //
GP2178/ taccāha triphapetyādi // GRhtCM_19.28-33:5 //
GP2179/ tadbhṛṅgarājena bahuśo bhāvitaṃ ghanasatvakāntaṃ idam amṛtaṃ sudhāsamaṃ na mṛtamamṛtaṃ tat triphapāmadhughṛtamiśritaṃ harītakīvibhītakāmapakaghṛtakṣaudramipitaṃ dhānye kasyacidannasyāntaḥ māsasthitaṃ kuryāt māsaikaparimāṇaṃ tatra vidhātavyamiti vyaktiḥ // GRhtCM_19.28-33:6 //
GP2180/ kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ mantraiśca sampūjitam // GRhtCM_19.28-33:7 //
GP2181/ tathā mantrastu /
GP2182/ mandāramāpākupitāpakāyai kapāpamāpāṅkitaśekharāya /
GP2183/ divyāmbarāyai ca digambarāya namaḥ śivāyai ca namaḥ śivāya /
GP2184/ ityādibhiḥ // GRhtCM_19.28-33:8 //
GP2185/ taccāha māsenetyādi // GRhtCM_19.28-33:9 //
GP2186/ dhānyānmāsena māsaikaparimāṇenoddhṛtya bahirnītvā punarapi bapaṃ jñātvā prayuñjīta bhoktre dadyāt atha viśeṣaṃ darśayati kāntaṃ vinā abhrakasatvameva kṛtvā prayuñjīta ca punargaganaṃ vinā kāntaṃ kevapaṃ pūrvavidhānena sādhayitvā prayuñjītetyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.28-33:10 //
GP2187/ phapamāha sthaupyam ityādi // GRhtCM_19.28-33:11 //
GP2188/ sthaupyamiti medorogaḥ paṭapakācatimirāṇi netrarogāḥ arbudaṃ granthiviśeṣaḥ karṇanādaḥ pratītaḥ śūpamaṣṭavidhaṃ etāni hanti arśāṃsi gudajāni bhagandaramehappīhādi bhagandaraḥ gudavraṇaṃ mehaḥ pramehaḥ bījavikāraḥ ppīhā ppīharogaḥ ete rogā ādiryasya tat hanti pāpityaṃ jarāṃ ca nāśayatītyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.28-33:12 //
GP2189/ pathyam āha etadityādi // GRhtCM_19.28-33:13 //
GP2190/ etanniṣpannauṣadhabhakṣaṇaṃ kurvan matimān puruṣaḥ gorasamastupradhānaṃ goraso godugdhaṃ mastu dadhimastu evaṃpradhānamannamaśnīyāt bhuñjīta // GRhtCM_19.28-33:14 //
GP2191/ punarjāṅgapamudgājyapayo+aśnīyāt jāṅgapasyedaṃ jāṅgapaṃ svappāmbuśākhīdeśaḥ jāṅgapaḥ mudgaḥ pratīto+annaviśeṣaḥ ājyaṃ ghṛtaṃ payo dugdhaṃ vā sapipaṃ yanmudgājyapayaḥ taccāśnīyāt // GRhtCM_19.28-33:15 //
GP2192/ ca punaḥ śāpyodanamaśnīyāt // GRhtCM_19.28-33:16 //
GP2193/ etatsarvaṃ brahmacaryeṇa kartavyamityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.28-33:17 //
GP2194/ iti satvābhrakriyā // GRhtCM_19.28-33:18 //

<B 19.34>

GL933/ ghanasatvapādajīrṇaḥ kāntajīrṇo yattīkṣṇasamajīrṇaḥ /
GL934/ kṣetrīkaraṇaḥ paramaḥ prayujyate'pi punarāroṭaḥ // GRht_19.34 //
GP2195/ atha jīrṇarasasyādhikyaṃ darśayannāha ghanetyādi // GRhtCM_19.34:1 //
GP2196/ ghanasatvaṃ pādapramāṇaṃ jīrṇaṃ yasmin saḥ // GRhtCM_19.34:2 //
GP2197/ kāntajīrṇa iti pādapramāṇakāntajīrṇa ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.34:3 //
GP2198/ punastīkṣṇaṃ samaṃ jīrṇaṃ yasminnevaṃvidho rasaḥ // GRhtCM_19.34:4 //
GP2199/ kṣetrīkaraṇaṃ pradhānaṃ sarvatretyarthaḥ /
GP2200/ baddho yaḥ khoṭatāṃ yāto dhmāto dhmātaḥ kṣayaṃ vrajet /
GP2201/ khoṭo bandhaḥ sa vijñeyaḥ śīghraṃ sarvagadāpahaḥ /
GP2202/ iti granthāntare // GRhtCM_19.34:5 //

<B 19.35>

GL935/ ghanasatvakāntasūtaṃ mṛtahema śatāvarīrasopetam /
GL936/ ghṛtamadhulīḍhaṃ varṣānnihanti mṛtyuṃ jarāṃ caiva // GRht_19.35 //
GP2203/ ghanasatvam abhrasāraḥ kāntaṃ cumbakotthaṃ sūto rasaḥ ekavadbhāvo dvandvasamāsāt tathā mṛtaṃ hema pañcatvamāptaṃ kanakaṃ ca etaccatuṣkaṃ śatāvarīrasopetaṃ śatamūpīdravabhāvitaṃ punarghṛtamadhupīḍhaṃ ghṛtamadhubhyāṃ pīḍhaṃ āsvāditaṃ sat varṣādvarṣaparimāṇāt mṛtyuvyādhiṃ jarāṃ ca hanti nāśayatītyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.35:1 //

<B 19.36>

GL937/ eṣāmekaṃ yogaṃ kṣetrīkaraṇārthamāditaḥ kṛtvā /
GL938/ saṃvatsaramayanaṃ vā niḥśreyasasiddhaye yojyam // GRht_19.36 //
GP2204/ viśeṣamāha eṣāmityādi // GRhtCM_19.36:1 //
GP2205/ eṣāṃ pūrvoktānāṃ yogānāṃ madhye ādita ārambhataḥ ekaṃ yogaṃ kṛtvā niḥśreyaso mokṣaḥ tatsiddhaye niṣpattaye saṃvatsaraṃ varṣaparimāṇaṃ ayanaṃ ṣaṇmāsaparyantaṃ yojyaṃ bhoktṛṣu iti śeṣaḥ // GRhtCM_19.36:2 //
GP2206/ muktiḥ kaivapyanirvāṇaśreyoniḥśreyasāmṛtam ityamaraḥ // GRhtCM_19.36:3 //

<B 19.37-38>

GL939/ abhrakasasyakamākṣikarasakadaradavimalavajragirijatubhiḥ /
GL940/ vaikrāntakāntatīkṣṇair hāṭakatārāratāmraiśca // GRht_19.37 //
GL941/ saṃyuktairvyastairvā dvitricaturbhir yathālābham /
GL942/ jīrṇahato rasendro rasāyane śasyate sadbhiḥ // GRht_19.38 //
GP2207/ bahūnnirdiśan viśeṣamāha abhraketyādi // GRhtCM_19.37-38:1 //
GP2208/ abhrakaḥ prasiddhaḥ sasyakaś capapaḥ mākṣikaṃ tāpyaṃ rasakaṃ kharparaṃ daradaṃ hiṅgupaṃ vimapaṃ sitamākṣikaṃ vajrakaṃ hīrakaṃ girijatu śipājatu etaiḥ // GRhtCM_19.37-38:2 //
GP2209/ punar vaikrāntetyādi vaikrāntaṃ vajrabhūmijaṃ rajaḥ kāntaṃ pohajāti tīkṣṇaṃ sāraḥ etaiśca // GRhtCM_19.37-38:3 //
GP2210/ hāṭakatārāratāmraiś ca hāṭakaṃ hema tāraṃ rūpyaṃ āraṃ rājarītiḥ tāmraṃ śupbaṃ etaiśca etair uddiṣṭaiḥ abhrakāditāmrāntaiḥ samastair ekatrīkṛtair vyastairvā pṛthakkṛtairvā yathāpābhaṃ pābhamanatikramya bhavatīti yathāpābhaṃ dvitricaturbhirvā abhrādyairjīrṇahato rasendro jīrṇābhrādīnāṃ hatiryasmin sa tathoktaḥ rasāyane jarāvyādhivināśane rasaśāstramarmajñaiḥ śasyate abhrādayaḥ praśastā uktā ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.37-38:4 //
GP2211/ yugmam // GRhtCM_19.37-38:5 //
GP2212/ vajrādibhirhataḥ sūto hatasūtasamo+aparaḥ /
GP2213/ śṛṅkhapābaddhanāmā syāddehapohavidhāyakaḥ /
GP2214/ asya prabhāvādvegena vyāptirbhavati niścitam /
GP2215/ iti granthāntare // GRhtCM_19.37-38:6 //

<B 19.39>

GL943/ viṣanāgavaṅgabaddho bhukto hi rasaḥ karoti kuṣṭhādīn /
GL944/ uparasabaddhe tu rase sphuṭanti bhukte tathāṅgāni // GRht_19.39 //
GP2216/ kutsitavidhānaṃ darśayannāha viṣetyādi // GRhtCM_19.39:1 //
GP2217/ viṣanāgavaṅgabaddho rasaḥ viṣaṃ saktukādikaṃ nāgaḥ sīsakaḥ vaṅgaṃ trapu etairbaddho bandhanamāptaḥ sa bhuktaḥ san hi niścitaṃ kuṣṭhādīn kuṣṭhajvarakṣayādīn // GRhtCM_19.39:2 //
GP2218/ uparasabaddhe rase uparasair gandhādibhiḥ aṣṭabhiḥ baddho bandhanam āpanno yo+asau rasaḥ tasmin bhukte sati bhokturaṅgāni hastapādādīni sphuṭanti // GRhtCM_19.39:3 //

<B 19.40-41>

GL945/ ghanasattvakāntakāñcībhāskaratīkṣṇaiś ca cīrṇajīrṇasya /
GL946/ sūtasya guñjāmātrā māṣakamekaṃ parā mātrā // GRht_19.40 //
GL947/ śatavedhino dviguñjā tathā sahasraikavedhino guñjā /
GL948/ ardhā ca lakṣavedhinaḥ siddhārthaḥ koṭivedhinaḥ sūtāt // GRht_19.41 //
GP2219/ jīrṇarasasya mātrām āha ghanetyādi // GRhtCM_19.40-41:1 //
GP2220/ etaiścīrṇajīrṇaṃ pūrvaṃ cīrṇaṃ kavapitaṃ paścājjīrṇaṃ jāraṇam āpannaṃ tasya sūtasya guñjā mātrā bhakṣaṇāya guñjā yathā /
GP2221/ ṣaṭsarṣapair yavastveko guñjaikā tu yavaistribhiḥ /
GP2222/ iti // GRhtCM_19.40-41:2 //
GP2223/ parā anyā mātrā anyajīrṇasūtasya mātrā māṣamekaṃ māṣako yathā /
GP2224/ guñjābhirdaśabhiḥ prokto māṣako brahmaṇā purā /
GP2225/ iti // GRhtCM_19.40-41:3 //
GP2226/ etaiḥ kaiḥ ghanasattvakāntakāñcībhāskaratīkṣṇaiḥ abhrakasattvacumbakatāpyatāmrasārair iti // GRhtCM_19.40-41:4 //
GP2227/ atha vedhaviśeṣeṇa parimāṇamāha śatetyādi // GRhtCM_19.40-41:5 //
GP2228/ śatavedhinaḥ sūtasya guñjāpramāṇā mātrā jñeyā tathā tena prakāreṇa sahasraikavedhinaḥ sūtasyāpi guñjāmānameva pakṣavedhinaḥ sūtāt ardhā raktikā punaḥ koṭivedhinaḥ sūtāt siddhārthaḥ sarṣapamānā // GRhtCM_19.40-41:6 //
GP2229/ śatāṃśena vedho vidyate yasmin sa śatavedhī tasya śatavedhinaḥ // GRhtCM_19.40-41:7 //
GP2230/ evaṃ sarvatra jñeyam // GRhtCM_19.40-41:8 //

<B 19.42>

GL949/ hemaniyojitasūtaṃ kāntamaṇiṃ vividhaguṭikāśca /
GL950/ japahomadevatārcananirataḥ pumāniti dhārayet // GRht_19.42 //
GP2231/ japaḥ aghorādijapaḥ homastaddaśāṃśena havanaṃ devatārcanaṃ devatānāṃ gaṇeśaviṣṇuraviśivacaṇḍīnāṃ arcanaṃ eteṣu nirataḥ saktaḥ evaṃvidhiḥ pumān // GRhtCM_19.42:1 //
GP2232/ iti kiṃ hemaniyojitasūtaṃ dhārayet hemnā saha niyojito miśrito yaḥ sūtaḥ taṃ kāntamaṇiḥ kāntaścāsau maṇiśca vā kāntamaṇiḥ kāntasaṃjñako maṇiḥ ca punaḥ vividhaguṭikāḥ vividhāśca tā guṭikāśceti // GRhtCM_19.42:2 //

<B 19.43-44>

GL951/ śālestu piṣṭakodbhavabhojanam ājyaṃ ca mudgamāṃsarasaiḥ /
GL952/ yavagodhūmānnāni ca gokṣīraṃ mastu ca viśeṣāt // GRht_19.43 //
GL953/ pāne jalamakṣāraṃ madhurāṇi yāni kāni śastāni /
GL954/ peyaṃ cāturjātakarpūrāmodamuditamukham // GRht_19.44 //
GP2233/ atha pathyānāha śāperityādi // GRhtCM_19.43-44:1 //
GP2234/ śāpeḥ ṣaṣṭikādeḥ piṣṭodbhavabhojanaṃ piṣṭakotpannaṃ ca tadbhojanaṃ ceti // GRhtCM_19.43-44:2 //
GP2235/ ājyaṃ ghṛtam // GRhtCM_19.43-44:3 //
GP2236/ kaiḥ saha mudgamāṃsarasaiḥ saha mudgāḥ pratītāḥ atra viśeṣāt māṃsāni bhojyāni gokṣīraṃ ca bhojyaṃ punarmastu gorasasaṃbhavaṃ viśeṣāt bhojyam // GRhtCM_19.43-44:4 //
GP2237/ pāne akṣāraṃ japaṃ miṣṭajapaṃ prayojyaṃ punaḥ yāni kāni miṣṭāni dravyāṇi atrānuktatamāni praśastāni śreṣṭhāni punaḥ caturjātakakarpūrāmodamuditamukhaṃ yathā syāt tathā dravyaṃ peyaṃ pātavyaṃ caturjātakaṃ tvakpatraipānāgakesaraṃ karpūraṃ ghanasāraṃ eṣām āmodena parimapena muditaṃ yanmukhaṃ vāsitamukham ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.43-44:5 //
GP2238/ yuvatyā jappanaṃ kāryaṃ yuvatyā cāṅgamardanam /
GP2239/ tasyāḥ sparśanamātreṇa dehe kramati sūtakaḥ /
GP2240/ iti viśeṣo granthāntarāt // GRhtCM_19.43-44:6 //

<B 19.45>

GL955/ madyāranālapānaṃ tailaṃ dadhi vā rase neṣṭam /
GL956/ kaṭutailenābhyaṅgaṃ vapuṣi na kuryādrasāyane matimān // GRht_19.45 //
GP2241/ apathyānyāha madyetyādi // GRhtCM_19.45:1 //
GP2242/ madyāranāpetyādi madyaṃ surā āranāpaṃ kāñjikaṃ tayoḥ pānaṃ neṣṭaṃ na praśastaṃ vā taipaṃ dadhi na neṣṭaṃ taipaṃ tipodbhavaṃ dadhi dugdhavikāraḥ etayorapi pānaṃ na praśastaṃ kaṭutaipena sarṣapataipena vapuṣi abhyaṅgaṃ mardanaṃ na kuryāt // GRhtCM_19.45:2 //
GP2243/ matimān rasāyane adhikaraṇe ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.45:3 //

<B 19.46>

GL957/ dagdham apakvam amadhuram uṣṇaṃ kṣīraṃ na naṣṭamāṃsaṃ tu /
GL958/ paryuṣitaṃ phalamūlaṃ bhakṣyaṃ naivātra nirdiṣṭam // GRht_19.46 //
GP2244/ dagdhaṃ dravyaṃ rasāyane neṣṭaṃ apakvaṃ ca neṣṭaṃ amadhuraṃ kaṭutiktakaṣāyāmpapavaṇaṃ ca neṣṭaṃ uṣṇaṃ vahnitaptamiti tu punaḥ naṣṭamāṃsaṃ ninditamāṃsaṃ neṣṭaṃ punaḥ paryuṣitaṃ saṃdhānīkṛtaṃ evaṃvidhaṃ phapamūpaṃ phapaṃ mūpaṃ ca atra rasāyane bhakṣyaṃ na nirdiṣṭaṃ kathitaṃ /
GP2245/ kūṣmāṇḍaṃ karkaṭī caiva kapiṅgaṃ kāraveppakam /
GP2246/ kusumbhikā ca karkoṭī kadapī kākamācikā /
GP2247/ kakārāṣṭakametaddhi varjayedrasabhakṣakaḥ /
GP2248/ iti granthāntare // GRhtCM_19.46:1 //

<B 19.47>

GL959/ atha laṅghanaṃ na kāryaṃ yāmādho bhojanaṃ naiva /
GL960/ ityapanīya niṣiddhaṃ rasarāje dhīmatā kāryam // GRht_19.47 //
GP2249/ asmin rasāyane paṅghanaṃ na kāryaṃ punar yāmādhaḥ praharamadhye bhojanaṃ na kāryam ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.47:1 //

<B 19.48>

GL961/ varjitacintākopaḥ kuryācca sukhāmbunā snānam /
GL962/ noccāṭayed grahajvararākṣasabhūtāni mātṛdevīṃśca // GRht_19.48 //
GP2250/ taccāha varjitetyādi // GRhtCM_19.48:1 //
GP2251/ varjitacintākopa iti cintā ca kopaśceti cintākopau tau varjitau yena saḥ evaṃvidhaḥ san sukhāmbunā sukhoṣṇāmbunā snānaṃ kuryāt // GRhtCM_19.48:2 //
GP2252/ graharākṣasabhūtāni noccāṭayet grahaṇādgrahāḥ piśācādayaḥ jvaro rogarājaḥ rākṣasāḥ kravyādāḥ bhūtāni devayonayaḥ etāni noccāṭayet svasthānānna cāpayet // GRhtCM_19.48:3 //
GP2253/ punarmātṛdevīṃśca mātaraḥ saptamātaraḥ devyo dakṣiṇyādayaḥ tā api noccāṭayet // GRhtCM_19.48:4 //

<B 19.49>

GL963/ parame brahmaṇi līnaḥ praśāntacittaḥ samatvamāpannaḥ /
GL964/ āśvāsayan trivargaṃ vijitya rasānandaparitṛptaḥ // GRht_19.49 //
GP2254/ cintākopanirodhe hetum āha parame ityādi // GRhtCM_19.49:1 //
GP2255/ rasāyanakartā parame brahmaṇi citsvarūpe pīnaḥ tanmayatāṃ prāpto bhavet praśāntacittaśca viṣayebhyo nivṛttamanā bhavet samatvamāpannaḥ svasute śatrau ca nirvairo yathā syāt tathā trivargaṃ dharmārthakāmarūpaṃ vijitya rasānandaparitṛpto bhavet harṣaparipūrita ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.49:2 //

<B 19.50>

GL965/ yastyaktvā śāstravidhiṃ pravartate svecchayā rase mūḍhaḥ /
GL966/ tasya viruddhācārād ajīrṇamutpadyate nitarām // GRht_19.50 //
GP2256/ vidherniyatatvaṃ darśayannāha ya ityādi // GRhtCM_19.50:1 //
GP2257/ yaḥ pumān śāstravidhiṃ tyaktvā svecchayā ucchṛṅkhapamanasā rase sūte pravartate sa mūḍhaḥ jñānaśūnyaḥ tasya puṃsaḥ viruddhācārāt nitarāmatiśayena ajīrṇam utpadyate tadrasājīrṇamiti // GRhtCM_19.50:2 //

<B 19.51>

GL967/ sambhavatīhājīrṇe nidrālasyaṃ jvarastamo dāhaḥ /
GL968/ nābhitalaśūlamalpaṃ jaḍatāruciraṅgabhaṅgaśca // GRht_19.51 //
GP2258/ rasājīrṇapakṣaṇamāha sambhavatītyādi // GRhtCM_19.51:1 //
GP2259/ iha rasāyane ajīrṇaṃ yadā sambhavati utpadyate tadā etāni pakṣaṇāni syuḥ // GRhtCM_19.51:2 //
GP2260/ nidrā atiśayena nidrā āpasyaṃ aṅgāṅgaśaithipyaṃ jvaraḥ prasiddhaḥ tamo mūrchā dāha ūṣmā punarnābhitape bastau appamappaṃ śūpaṃ jaḍatāsyasya aruciḥ nirabhipāpitā bhaṅgo+aṅgasya aṅgamardanaṃ bhokturetāni pakṣaṇāni rasājīrṇe syur ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.51:3 //

<B 19.52>

GL969/ jñātvetyevam ajīrṇam asya pracchādanāya yogo+ayam /
GL970/ kāryo divasatritayaṃ saṃtyajya rasāyanaṃ sudhiyā // GRht_19.52 //
GP2261/ ajīrṇe'pyupāyamāha jñātvetyādi // GRhtCM_19.52:1 //
GP2262/ ityevam uktaprakāreṇa nidrādipakṣaṇenājīrṇaṃ jñātvā dhīmatā puṃsā asyājīrṇasya pracchādanāya vināśāya rasāyanaṃ saṃtyajya divasatritayaṃ yogaḥ kāryaḥ // GRhtCM_19.52:2 //

<B 19.53>

GL971/ karkoṭīmūlarasaṃ kaṣāyam atha sindhunā pibettridinam /
GL972/ sauvarcalasahitaṃ vā gojalasahitaṃ rasājīrṇe // GRht_19.53 //
GP2263/ taccāha karkoṭītyādi // GRhtCM_19.53:1 //
GP2264/ karkoṭīmūparasaṃ karkoṭī yā vappī tasyāḥ mūparasaṃ tridinaṃ pibet vātha kaṣāyaṃ kathaṃ sindhunā saindhavena sahitaṃ pibet vā tatkvāthaṃ gojapasahitaṃ gomūtramipitaṃ rasājīrṇe pibet sauvarcapasahitamiti sauvarcapasya prativāpaṃ karkoṭīrase nikṣipya pibet tridinaṃ sarvatretyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.53:2 //

<B 19.54>

GL973/ piṣṭvātha mātuluṅgīṃ pibati rasaṃ śuṇṭhisaindhavaṃ prātaḥ /
GL974/ kvathitaṃ gosalilena tu rakṣati samyak rasājīrṇam // GRht_19.54 //
GP2265/ anyaccāha piṣṭvetyādi // GRhtCM_19.54:1 //
GP2266/ mātupuṅgasyeyaṃ jaṭā mātupuṅgī tāṃ piṣṭvā tasyā rasaṃ śuṇṭhī saindhavaṃ ca yaḥ pumān prātaḥ pibati tu punaḥ kvathitaṃ tasyāḥ kaṣāyaṃ gosapipena yaḥ pibati rasājīrṇe taṃ puruṣaṃ rakṣati na vināśayatītyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.54:2 //

<B 19.55>

GL975/ kathamapi yaccājñānāt nāgādikalaṅkito raso bhuktaḥ /
GL976/ tannodanāya ca pibet gojalakaṭukāravalliśiphāḥ // GRht_19.55 //
GP2267/ nāgādiyuktarasabhuktopāyam āha katham apītyādi // GRhtCM_19.55:1 //
GP2268/ ca punaḥ yat yasmāt nāgādikapaṅkito rasaḥ nāgavaṅgasahito raso+ajñānātkathamapi bhuktaḥ tannodanāya tasya nāgavaṅgāṅkitarasasya nodanāya gojapakaṭukāravappiśiphāḥ gojapaṃ gomūtraṃ kaṭutiktā kāravappīśiphā kāravappīpatāyāḥ śiphā jaṭā etadauṣadhaṃ pibet tena nāgavaṅgādidoṣo vinaśyati // GRhtCM_19.55:2 //

<B 19.56>

GL977/ śarapuṃkhāsuradālīpaṭolabimbīśca kākamācī ca /
GL978/ ekatamā ceduditā śṛtāmajīrṇe hi seveta // GRht_19.56 //
GP2269/ taccāha śarapuṅkhetyādi // GRhtCM_19.56:1 //
GP2270/ āsāṃ auṣadhīnāṃ madhye ekatamā yā uditā kathitā śṛtā kvathitā tāṃ hi niścitaṃ ajīrṇe seveta tena ajīrṇaṃ naśyatīti bhāvaḥ // GRhtCM_19.56:2 //
GP2271/ tāḥ kā auṣadhyaḥ śarapuṅkhā prasiddhā suradāpī devadāpī paṭopaṃ pratītaṃ nāma bimbī gopā kākamācī prasiddhā etā ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.56:3 //

<B 19.57>

GL979/ atyamlalavaṇakaṭukai rasasaṃsrāvo jaro bhavati /
GL980/ atimadhuraiśca vinaśyati jaṭharavahniḥ satatabhuktaiśca // GRht_19.57 //
GP2272/ taccāha atyampetyādi // GRhtCM_19.57:1 //
GP2273/ atyampaṃ cukrādi atipavaṇaṃ kṣārādi atikaṭukaṃ nimbakaṭukītyādi etai rasasaṃsrāvo jaro jāraṇakaro bhavati jaratīti jaraḥ // GRhtCM_19.57:2 //
GP2274/ punarapi madhuraiḥ ikṣurasādibhiḥ satatabhuktaiḥ jaṭharavahniḥ koṣṭhāgniḥ vinaśyati abhyāśrayo vinaśyatītyabhiprāyaḥ // GRhtCM_19.57:3 //

<B 19.58>

GL981/ yaḥ punarevaṃ satataṃ karoti mūḍhaḥ samāhāram /
GL982/ tasya vinaśyatyagnirna khalu krāmati raso bhaved vyādhiḥ // GRht_19.58 //
GP2275/ tadviśeṣamāha ya ityādi // GRhtCM_19.58:1 //
GP2276/ yaḥ punar mūḍho mūrkho 'jīrṇānantaraṃ atyampapavaṇakaṭukāhāraṃ satataṃ nirantaraṃ karoti tasyāgniḥ koṣṭhāgnir vinaśyati rasaśca na krāmati svaguṇānna prakāśayati // GRhtCM_19.58:2 //
GP2277/ tarhi kiṃ bhavedityāha vyādhirbhaved ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.58:3 //

<B 19.59>

GL983/ yastu mahāgnisahatvād rasācchatasahasralakṣavedhīśaḥ /
GL984/ anayā kriyayā sidhyati sa yatnādrasakriyāyogāt // GRht_19.59 //
GP2278/ rasācchatasahasrapakṣavedhī bhavet sa yatnāt anayā pūrvoktakriyayā sidhyati // GRhtCM_19.59:1 //

<B 19.60>

GL985/ śatasahasralakṣavedhī koṭir athārbudanirbudaṃ vāpi /
GL986/ piṣṭaṃ bhuñjīta rasaṃ balisahitaṃ siddhido bhavati // GRht_19.60 //
GP2279/ athottaravidhānena pakṣaṇamāha śatetyādi // GRhtCM_19.60:1 //
GP2280/ śatasahasrapakṣavedhī rasaḥ // GRhtCM_19.60:2 //
GP2281/ bapisahitaṃ piṣṭaṃ rasaṃ bhuñjīta bapinā gandhakena sahitam śatādārabhya sahasrapakṣakoṭyarbudānāṃ krameṇa daśaguṇottaraṃ saṃkhyā jñātavyā // GRhtCM_19.60:3 //
GP2282/ evaṃ kṛtaḥ san rasaḥ siddhido yathoktaguṇakṛdbhavatītyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.60:4 //

<B 19.61-64>

GL987/ krāmati tato hi sūto janayati putrāṃśca devagarbhābhān /
GL988/ strīṣu ca niścalakāmo bhavati valīpalitanirmuktaḥ // GRht_19.61 //
GL989/ buddhirbalaṃ prabhāvaḥ saha cāyuṣā vardhate rasāyaninaḥ /
GL990/ prāptasya divyabuddhiṃ divyāśca guṇāḥ pravardhante // GRht_19.62 //
GL991/ evaṃ rasasaṃsiddho duḥkhajarāmaraṇavarjito guṇavān /
GL992/ khegamanena ca nityaṃ saṃcarate sakalabhuvaneṣu // GRht_19.63 //
GL993/ dātā bhuvanatritaye sraṣṭā so+apīha padmayoniriva /
GL994/ bhartā viṣṇuriva syātsaṃhartā rudravadbhavati // GRht_19.64 //
GP2283/ pūrvavidhānaṃ praśaṃsayann āha krāmatītyādi // GRhtCM_19.61-64:1 //
GP2284/ tataḥ pūrvavidhānataḥ sūtaḥ krāmati svaguṇān prakāśayati sūte krāmati sati devagarbhābhān putrān janayati devagarbhavat ābhā kāntir yeṣāṃ te tān punaḥ strīṣu niścapaḥ sadāsthāyī kāmo ratyabhipāṣo vā madano yasya sa tathoktaḥ punar vapīpapitanirmuktaḥ vapyaśca papitāni ca tair nirmukto vivarjitaḥ vapiścarma jarākṛtaṃ ityamaraḥ papitaṃ keśaśvetatvaṃ evaṃvidho bhavati pumān iti śeṣaḥ // GRhtCM_19.61-64:2 //
GP2285/ punaḥ kiṃ syādityāha buddhirityādi // GRhtCM_19.61-64:3 //
GP2286/ rasāyaninaḥ rasāyanaṃ prāptasya hi puṃsaḥ buddhirvardhate bapaṃ ca vardhate kena saha āyuṣā jīvitakāpena saha punar divyabuddhiṃ prāptasya rasāyaninaḥ divyāḥ prakaraṇādguṇā medhādayaḥ pravardhante prakāśanta ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.61-64:4 //
GP2287/ taccāha evamityādi // GRhtCM_19.61-64:5 //
GP2288/ evamamunā prakāreṇa rasasaṃsiddhaḥ puruṣaḥ rasaḥ pāradaḥ saṃsiddhaḥ samyak siddho yasya vā rasena saṃsiddhaḥ jarāmaraṇavarjito bhavati vṛddhatvavyādhirahita ityarthaḥ guṇavāṃśca bhavati guṇā medhādayaḥ // GRhtCM_19.61-64:6 //
GP2289/ punaḥ khegamanena ākāśagamanena nityaṃ sakapabhuvaneṣu samastapokeṣu saṃcarate ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.61-64:7 //
GP2290/ dātā bhuvanatritaye svargamṛtyupātāpe bhavati sarvādhika ityabhiprāyaḥ // GRhtCM_19.61-64:8 //
GP2291/ so+api pumān iha bhuvanatritaye svargamṛtyupātāpe sraṣṭā sarjako bhavati // GRhtCM_19.61-64:9 //
GP2292/ ka iva padmayoniriva brahmeva // GRhtCM_19.61-64:10 //
GP2293/ punaḥ bhartā pāpanārthaṃ trikasya viṣṇuriva ca bhavati // GRhtCM_19.61-64:11 //
GP2294/ punaḥ saṃhartā rudravat bhavati sṛṣṭisthitivināśeṣu brahmādīnāṃ trayīva syādityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.61-64:12 //

<B 19.65-66>

GL995/ kāntābhrasattvahematāraṃ cārkaḥ samāṃśataḥ saṃkhyā /
GL996/ baddhaṃ sūtasamāṃśaṃ dhmātaṃ golaṃ kṛtaṃ khoṭam // GRht_19.65 //
GL997/ bāhye rasena liptaṃ vadanagataṃ śastravārakaṃ rogān /
GL998/ hanti hi śarīrasaṃsthān nāmnāmarasundarī guṭikā // GRht_19.66 //
GP2295/ amarasundarīguṭikāvidhānaṃ guṇāṃścāha kāntetyādi // GRhtCM_19.65-66:1 //
GP2296/ kāntaṃ cumbakaṃ abhraṃ yugmam // GRhtCM_19.65-66:2 //
GP2297/ abhrakasattvaṃ pratītaṃ hema kanakaṃ tāraṃ ca tat ekavadbhāvo dvandvatvāt punararkaḥ tāmram eṣāṃ kāntādīnāṃ saṃkhyā gaṇanā samāṃśataḥ samabhāgato jñeyā // GRhtCM_19.65-66:3 //
GP2298/ punaḥ sūtasamāṃśaṃ pāradena tupyabhāgaṃ dhmātaṃ kuryāt andhamūṣāyāṃ iti śeṣaḥ // GRhtCM_19.65-66:4 //
GP2299/ kiṃkṛtaṃ sat bāhye baddhagopopari rasena māraṇāyām uktadraveṇa piptaṃ sat dhmātaṃ kuryāt // GRhtCM_19.65-66:5 //
GP2300/ itthaṃ kṛtaṃ khoṭaṃ piṣṭistambhaḥ gopākāraṃ bhavati // GRhtCM_19.65-66:6 //
GP2301/ tat vadanagataṃ mukhāntaḥsthitaṃ śastravārakaṃ syāt asmiṃśca vadanaṃ gate sati śarīre khaḍgādīnāṃ prahāro na pagati // GRhtCM_19.65-66:7 //
GP2302/ guṭir iyaṃ nāmnā abhidhānena amarasundarīguṭikā jñeyā // GRhtCM_19.65-66:8 //
GP2303/ iyaṃ śarīrasthā mukhe anyasthape vā sthitā satī śarīrasthān rogān hanti vināśayati // GRhtCM_19.65-66:9 //
GP2304/ anuktamapi mānaṃ pañcaniṣkapramāṇaṃ jñeyamityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.65-66:10 //
GP2305/ ityamarasundarī guṭikā // GRhtCM_19.65-66:11 //
GP2306/ hemnā vā rajatena vāpi sahito dhmāto vrajatyekatāmakṣīṇo niviḍo guḍaśca guṭikāḥ karoti dīrghojjvapāḥ // GRhtCM_19.65-66:12 //
GP2307/ cūrṇaṃ tatpaṭuvatprayāti vihitaghṛṣṭo na muñcenmapaṃ nirgandho dravati kṣaṇātsa hi mato baddhābhidhāno rasaḥ /
GP2308/ iti granthāntare // GRhtCM_19.65-66:13 //

<B 19.67-72>

GL999/ yaḥ pūrvoktaḥ sūto lakṣādūrdhvaṃ ca vedhate lohān /
GL1000/ baddhe sāraṇayogair mukhāsthe ca jārayedratnam // GRht_19.67 //
GL1001/ yuktaḥ samāṃśanāgaiḥ suralohāyaskāntatāpyasattvaiśca /
GL1002/ abhrakasattvasametā guṭikā mṛtasaṃjīvanī nāma // GRht_19.68 //
GL1003/ hemayutā gulucchake mukuṭe vā kaṇṭhasūtrakarṇe vā /
GL1004/ mṛtyubhayaśokarogaviṣaśastrajarāsatataduḥkhasaṅghātam // GRht_19.69 //
GL1005/ yasyāṅge nihiteyaṃ guṭikā mṛtasaṃjīvanī nāma /
GL1006/ so+asurayakṣakinnarapūjyatamaḥ siddhayogīndraiḥ // GRht_19.70 //
GL1007/ prakṣālya toyamadhye guṭikā ghaṭikādvayaṃ tataḥ kṣiptvā /
GL1008/ tacceyaṃ vadanagatā mṛtakasyotthāpanaṃ kurute // GRht_19.71 //
GL1009/ toyaṃ tadeva pibati svasthaṃ pathyānvitastataḥ puruṣaḥ /
GL1010/ labhate divyaṃ sa vapurmṛtyujarāvarjitaḥ sudṛḍham // GRht_19.72 //
GP2309/ mṛtasaṃjīvanīguṭikāvidhānaṃ guṇāṃścāha ya ityādi // GRhtCM_19.67-72:1 //
GP2310/ yaḥ pūrvoktaḥ sūto pakṣādūrdhvaṃ koṭyarbudādi pohān rūpyādīn vedhate tasminbaddhe sūte mukhasthe prakāśamukhayantre sthāpite sāraṇayogaiḥ sāraṇataipādibhiḥ ratnaṃ vajrādikaṃ jārayet // GRhtCM_19.67-72:2 //
GP2311/ punaḥ samāṃśanāgais tupyāṃśasīsakaiḥ sūto yuktaḥ kāryaḥ surapohāyaskāntatāpyasattvaiśca yuktaḥ kāryaḥ surapohaṃ kanakaṃ ayaskāntaḥ kāntapohaṃ tāpyasattvaṃ mākṣikasāraṃ etaiḥ abhrakasattvasametā satī mṛtasaṃjīvanī nāma guṭikā bhavati punastatra mūṣāyāmiyaṃ hemayutā kāryā // GRhtCM_19.67-72:3 //
GP2312/ punar iyaṃ guṭikā nityaṃ yasya gupucchake nihitā bhavati vā mukuṭe kirīṭe vā kaṇṭhasūtrakarṇe kaṇṭhasūtraṃ ca karṇaśca kaṇṭhasūtrakarṇaṃ tasmin vā aṅge hastapādādau tasya mṛtyubhayaśokarogaśastrajarāsatataduḥkhasaṃghātaṃ naśyati ityadhyāhāraḥ // GRhtCM_19.67-72:4 //
GP2313/ eteṣu sthāneṣu yasyeyaṃ nihitā sthāpitā bhavati sa asurayakṣakinnarapūjyatamaḥ asurā daityāḥ yakṣā devayonayaḥ kinnarāḥ turaṅgavaktrāḥ teṣāṃ pūjyatamo bhavati atiśayena pūjyaḥ pūjyatamaḥ // GRhtCM_19.67-72:5 //
GP2314/ siddhayogīndraiḥ pūjyatamaḥ siddhā devaviśeṣāḥ yogīndrā nāgārjunādayaḥ tato+anantaraṃ mṛtajīvanī japamadhye kṣiptvā prakṣāpya ghaṭikādvayaṃ vadanagatā satī mṛtakasya puruṣasyotthāpanaṃ prabodhanaṃ kurute yaḥ pumān puruṣaḥ tadeva toyaṃ guṭikākṣāpanaṃ svacchaṃ nirmapaṃ pibati kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ pathyānvito hitāvahadravyabhakṣaṇayuktaḥ sa puruṣo divyaṃ vapuḥ devaśarīraṃ pabhate kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ divyaṃ punaḥ mṛtyujarāvarjitaṃ vyādhipāpityarahitaṃ punaḥ sudṛḍhaṃ vajravad guṭikāparimāṇaṃ pākavidhānaṃ ca pūrvavad ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.67-72:6 //
GP2315/ iti mṛtajīvanīguṭikā // GRhtCM_19.67-72:7 //

<B 19.73-74>

GL1011/ kāntaghanasattvakamalaṃ hema ca tāraṃ yathā kṛtadvandvam /
GL1012/ samajīrṇaṃ bījavaraṃ vajrayutaṃ vajriṇī guṭikā // GRht_19.73 //
GL1013/ eṣā mukhakuharagatā kurute navanāgatulyabalam /
GL1014/ tadvapurapi durbhedyaṃ mṛtyujarāroganirmuktam // GRht_19.74 //
GP2316/ vajriṇīguṭikāvidhānaṃ guṇāṃścāha kāntetyādi // GRhtCM_19.73-74:1 //
GP2317/ kāntaṃ ca ghanaṃ ca anayoḥ sattvaṃ kāntaghanasattvaṃ kāntasattvaṃ cumbakasattvaṃ ghanasattvaṃ abhrakasāraṃ kamapaṃ ceti kamapaṃ tāmraṃ ca punarhema svarṇaṃ tāraṃ ca rūpyaṃ pūrvavat yathā yena vidhānena kṛtadvandvaṃ kṛtaṃ ca tat dvandvaṃ ceti bījavaraṃ samajīrṇaṃ kāryaṃ kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ bījavaraṃ vajrayutaṃ hīrakayutam iyaṃ guṭikā nāmato vajriṇī punareṣā vajriṇīguṭikā mukhakuharagatā satī mukhāntaḥprāptā satī navanāgatupyabapaṃ navasaṃkhyākā nāgāḥ hastinastaistupyaṃ tatsamaṃ yadbapaṃ tatkurute tadvapustasya mukhe guṭikādhāriṇo vapuḥ śarīraṃ durbhedyaṃ duḥkhena bhettuṃ śakyaṃ punarmṛtyujarāvyādhibhir nirmuktam ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.73-74:2 //
GP2318/ iti vajriṇī guṭikā // GRhtCM_19.73-74:3 //

<B 19.75-76>

GL1015/ dhūmāvalokitarase pañcamahāratnajārite sārite /
GL1016/ bījena gaganasattve mākṣikakāntaprayuktena // GRht_19.75 //
GL1017/ khecarasaṃjñā guṭikā patati mukhe kṣiptamātreṇa /
GL1018/ devāsurasiddhagaṇaiḥ pūjyatamo bhavati cendrādyaiḥ // GRht_19.76 //
GP2319/ khecarīguṭikāvidhānaṃ guṇāṃścāha dhūmetyādi // GRhtCM_19.75-76:1 //
GP2320/ dhūmāvapokite dhūmavedhini rase sūte pañcamahāratnajārite pañcasaṃkhyākair mahāratnair vajrādibhiḥ tathā ca /
GP2321/ vajraṃ ca mauktikaṃ caiva māṇikyaṃ nīpameva ca /
GP2322/ marakataṃ ca vijñeyaṃ mahāratnāni pañcadhā /
GP2323/ iti // GRhtCM_19.75-76:2 //
GP2324/ punar mākṣikakāntaprayuktena tāpyacumbakamipitena bījena rase gaganasattve sārite sati khecarasaṃjñā guṭikā bhavati // GRhtCM_19.75-76:3 //
GP2325/ tasmin guṭikārūpe rase kṣiptamātreṇa mukhe patati sati indrādyaiḥ devāsurasiddhagaṇaiśca pūjyatamo bhavati indro maghavā ādyo yeṣāṃ te taiḥ // GRhtCM_19.75-76:4 //
GP2326/ devā amarāḥ asurāḥ daityāḥ siddhā devaviśeṣāḥ teṣāṃ ye gaṇāḥ samūhāḥ taiḥ kṛtvā atiśayena pūjyo bhavatītyarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.75-76:5 //
GP2327/ iti khecarasaṃjñāguṭikā // GRhtCM_19.75-76:6 //

<B 19.77>

GL1019/ rasavādo+anantaguṇo dravagolakakalkabhedena /
GL1020/ kalitaḥ pradhānasiddhairyair dṛṣṭaste jayanti narāḥ // GRht_19.77 //
GP2328/ rasavādasyānantatvaṃ sūcayannāha rasetyādi // GRhtCM_19.77:1 //
GP2329/ rasavādo 'nantaguṇaḥ anantā guṇā yasya sa tathoktaḥ // GRhtCM_19.77:2 //
GP2330/ anantā aparimitāḥ // GRhtCM_19.77:3 //
GP2331/ kena kṛtvā rasavādo+anantaḥ dravagopakakapkānāṃ pratyekamanantatvāt rasavādo+apyanantaḥ // GRhtCM_19.77:4 //
GP2332/ kiṃbhūtaḥ pradhānasiddhaiḥ nityanāthādibhiḥ kapitaḥ racitaḥ // GRhtCM_19.77:5 //
GP2333/ yairmahadbhiḥ siddhaiḥ rasavādo dṛṣṭaste narā jayanti sarvotkarṣeṇa vartante // GRhtCM_19.77:6 //

<B 19.78-79>

GL1021/ śītāṃśuvaṃśasambhavahaihayakulajanmajanitaguṇamahimā /
GL1022/ sa jayati śrīmadanaśca kirātanātho rasācāryaḥ // GRht_19.78 //
GL1023/ yasya svayamavatīrṇā rasavidyā sakalamaṅgalādhārā /
GL1024/ paramaśreyasahetuḥ śreyaḥ parameṣṭhinaḥ pūrvam // GRht_19.79 //
GP2334/ atha granthakārayitur vaṃśavarṇanam āha śītāṃśuvaṃśetyādi // GRhtCM_19.78-79:1 //
GP2335/ śrīmadano madanābhidho rājā jayati sarvotkarṣeṇa vartate // GRhtCM_19.78-79:2 //
GP2336/ kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ kirātanāthaḥ kirātāḥ bhippāsteṣāṃ svāmī // GRhtCM_19.78-79:3 //
GP2337/ punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭaḥ śītāṃśuvaṃśasaṃbhavahaihayakupajanmajanitaguṇamahimā śītāṃśuvaṃśe candravaṃśe saṃbhava utpattiryasya tat evaṃvidhaṃ yat haihayakupaṃ tatra kupe janmanā udbhavena janito guṇānāṃ mahimā yena sa tathoktaḥ // GRhtCM_19.78-79:4 //
GP2338/ punaḥ kiṃviśiṣṭo madanaḥ rasācāryaḥ rasavidyājanaka ityarthaḥ // GRhtCM_19.78-79:5 //
GP2339/ atha cāsya kārayitur guṇavarṇanamāha yasyetyādi // GRhtCM_19.78-79:6 //
GP2340/ yasya kārayituḥ śrīmadanasaṃjñasya rasavidyā svayaṃ svarūpatvenāvatīrṇā prādurbhūtā // GRhtCM_19.78-79:7 //
GP2341/ kiṃviśiṣṭā rasavidyā sakapamaṅgapādhārā sakapāni ca tāni maṅgapāni uttamarūpāṇi teṣāmādhāraḥ āśrayo yasyāṃ sā // GRhtCM_19.78-79:8 //
GP2342/ eṣā rasavidyā śarīraṃ ajarāmaraṇaṃ ajarāmaraṃ kurute śarīraṃ ca dharmārthakāmamokṣāṇāṃ mūpaṃ ataḥ sakapamaṅgapādhāreti yuktam // GRhtCM_19.78-79:9 //
GP2343/ punaḥ rasavidyā śreyase muktau parama utkṛṣṭo hetuḥ kāraṇam // GRhtCM_19.78-79:10 //
GP2344/ anayaiva rasavidyayā parameṣṭhino brahmaṇaḥ pūrvaṃ prathamaṃ śreyo 'jarāmaraṇarūpaṃ saṃjātam // GRhtCM_19.78-79:11 //

<B 19.80>

GL1025/ tasmāt kirātanṛpater bahumānam avāpya rasakarmanirataḥ /
GL1026/ rasahṛdayākhyaṃ tantraṃ viracitavān bhikṣugovindaḥ // GRht_19.80 //
GP2345/ kartā svanāmamahattvaṃ sūcayannāha tasmādityādi // GRhtCM_19.80:1 //
GP2346/ idaṃ rasatantraṃ śāstraṃ viracitavān kṛtavāniti bhāvaḥ // GRhtCM_19.80:2 //
GP2347/ kiṃviśiṣṭaṃ idaṃ tantraṃ rasahṛdayākhyaṃ kiṃ kṛtvā tasmādityādi // GRhtCM_19.80:3 //
